### 27.01.2026 16:57  [Александр Семенов](https://app2top.ru/author/admin)

# «Суммарно опубликовали 24 тысяч игр, а сняли 29 тысяч», — Никита Бокарев из «Яндекс Игры» об итогах 2025 года

### Опубликовано:  [Александр Семенов](https://app2top.ru/author/admin)

### Теги:

- [итоги 2025](https://app2top.ru/tag/2025-in-video-games),
- [Яндекс.Игры](https://app2top.ru/tag/yandeks-igry)

Уже в 2026 году продолжаем подводить итоги 2025 года с игровыми (или связанными с игровым сектором) командами и экспертами. В этот раз редакция поговорила с Никитой Бокаревым, руководителем по развитию бизнеса « [Яндекс Игры](https://yandex.ru/games/)».

## ![](https://app2top.ru/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/YAndeks-Igry-.jpg)

## Каким 2025 год оказался для вашей игровой платформы с точки зрения бизнеса?

Мы преодолели отметку в 50 млн активных игроков в месяц и выросли на 10% по сравнению с 2024 годом, а наш ключевой фокус полностью сместился на качество игр и сервиса в целом.

Благодаря этому выручка разработчиков от внутриигровых покупок уже выросла на 75% по сравнению с 2024 годом.

Можно подвести такой промежуточный итог: мы растем за счет повышения качества контента и распространения внутриигровых покупок.

## Расскажите, как поменялась платформа с продуктовой стороны за год? И почему конкретные изменения были имплементированы?

Все изменения на платформе происходят, потому что мы хотим дать долгосрочную ценность игрокам и устойчивый рост дохода разработчикам.

![](https://app2top.ru/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/1-2.png)

Мы переработали и улучшили рекомендации, чтобы игрок быстрее находил «свою» игру, а разработчик – целевую аудиторию. Но если и этого будет мало, теперь у нас есть ИИ-ассистент, который помогает игрокам искать интересные проекты.

Главную страницу сервиса ждет большое будущее: пока что мы поменяли дизайн и интерфейс, а также стали уделять больше внимания игровым ивентам и LiveOps.

Игр на платформе стало меньше, потому что мы ввели новый алгоритм оценки игр и убрали из каталога около 10 тысяч непопулярных проектов с низким рейтингом. Сейчас в каталоге 19 тысяч игр: суммарно за год опубликовали 24 тысяч игр, а сняли 29 тысяч. При этом мы видим стабильный рост количества казуальных и мидкорных игр.

Мы все еще открыты для самых разных проектов, просто планка качества стала выше.

## Какие ключевые изменения произошли на платформе с точки зрения представленных продуктов/рыночных трендов/жанров?

Главный тренд 2025 года, который мы наблюдали на собственных данных – «мидкор» вошел в топ-5 самых популярных жанров на «Яндекс Играх». Сейчас на платформе успешно живут более тяжелые игры с глубоким геймплеем, более качественной графикой и гибридной монетизацией.

![](https://app2top.ru/wp-content/uploads/2026/01/2-2.png)

Тренд на кроссплатформенность и портирование мобильных хитов (который мы отмечали в 2024-ом) окончательно сформировался и подтвердился. Мы видим приток игр, которые всего пару лет назад были немыслимы в вебе.

Это стратегии, глубокие RPG, сложные симуляторы. Такие студии как Red Brix Wall, TAPCLAP, HeroCraft, VIZOR Games, Helio Games принесли на платформу проекты, которые меняют само представление о возможностях браузера. Их успех доказывает: аудитория веба готова к долгосрочным проектам и готова в них тратить деньги.

## Изменилось поведение игрока за 2025 год?

Да, поведение игроков продолжает трансформироваться вместе с изменениями на платформе.

Мы видим рост среднего плейтайма, он уверенно приближается к 60 минутам. Игроки осознанно приходят в определенные проекты, в которые они глубоко погружены, а не выбирают случайные гиперказуальные игры для коротких сессий.

Платежи стали привычной частью игрового опыта — это подтверждают сами игроки. Они покупают бонусы, бустеры, пропуска или элементы кастомизации. Веб окончательно стал для них еще одной полноценной игровой платформой, где системы монетизации зачастую не отличаются от нативных мобильных апов.

## Усиление или появление каких трендов в рамках платформы ждете в 2026 году?

Мы ожидаем усиления всех трендов, которые проявились в 2025-ом.

1. Доминирование гибридной монетизации. Доля платежей будет только расти. Модель «реклама \+ инапы» станет стандартом для проектов определенных жанров на вебе.
2. Разнообразие жанров. В веб придет еще больше игр тех жанров, которые сегодня встречаются редко: файтинги, сетевые шутеры, масштабные стратегии, комплексные RPG, кооперативы и другие.
3. Технологический рывок. С развитием движков и поддержкой WebGPU граница между вебом и нативными платформами продолжит стираться.

## Чего ждать от платформы в 2026 году?

Мы продолжим делать платформу более технологичной и удобной для разработчиков, чтобы выходить в веб было еще проще. В планах — расширение и усиление инструментов, которые помогут управлять играми и влиять на их успех.

Мы продолжим развивать и масштабировать направление мидкорных игр с внутриигровыми покупками. Это наша стратегическая ставка, и мы видим, что рынок движется в том же направлении.

И самое главное – мы продолжим работать в плотной связке с нашим сообществом разработчиков.

* * *

I'm thinking through the design for a game I'm calling «РОЙ» (The Swarm) — it's a Vampire Survivors-like where you control an auto-attacking entity that grows in size, with a Russian-language theme and one-button controls.

The core mechanic is that you're a single cell in a primordial ocean, enemies are hostile organisms, XP are food pellets, and leveling up triggers evolution. I'm mapping out the full design doc now to keep scope tight while making it feel polished. The game has a title screen with meta progression (spending biomass on permanent upgrades like speed, HP, magnetism, and damage), then runs through a 15-minute campaign with waves every minute, elite enemies every 3 minutes, and bosses at 3 and 6 minutes. Weapons and evolutions unlock as you level, with a run summary screen at the end showing stats and offering an ad-revive option. The weapon progression is what drives the addictive moment-to-moment decisions.

I'm laying out the starting weapons: a basic spike that fires at the nearest enemy, a passive orbiting body that damages on contact, an expanding shockwave that slows, a bouncing projectile, and a short-lived swarm burst every 8 seconds. Then there are higher-tier evolutions that combine weapons with passive items—like the spike evolving into a homing needle burst when paired with speed, or the orbit evolving into a larger rotating shield with knockback.

I'm also defining passive upgrades like movement speed, max health with healing, damage multiplier, cooldown reduction, projectile count, and XP magnetism, with each stat capped at 5 levels.

For enemies, I'm setting up chasers that move directly at you, fast erratic darters, splitting blobs that divide into smaller pieces, shooters that fire projectiles, and larger tank enemies.

The real retention hooks are the dopamine of constant level-ups with a shrinking XP bar, the strategic tension of picking the right build at each level, the escalating visual and audio intensity, and the permanent meta progression that makes you stronger each run so you always want to try again.

I'm also factoring in the daily reset bonuses for a 2× first run, ad placements that reward players without forcing them, leaderboards for competitive play, and achievements that unlock new characters. I want to layer in haptic feedback through canvas shake, hitstop on impact, and particle effects to make everything feel snappy and responsive.

[tool: write_file]

input.path:
/home/user/yandex-game/js/sdk.js
input.content:
/**
 * sdk.js — Yandex Games SDK adapter.
 *
 * Implements the calls that Yandex moderation actually checks:
 *   1. YaGames.init() -> ysdk
 *   2. ysdk.features.LoadingAPI.ready()   (without this the platform loading screen never hides)
 *   3. ysdk.features.GameplayAPI.start()/stop()  (stop BEFORE every ad, start after)
 *   4. ysdk.on('game_api_pause'|'game_api_resume') -> pause game + mute audio
 *   5. ysdk.adv.showFullscreenAdv / showRewardedVideo / sticky banner
 *   6. ysdk.getPlayer() + player.getData()/setData() for cloud save (offline-earnings, meta upgrades)
 *   7. ysdk.getLeaderboards() for the global best-run board
 *   8. ysdk.events / reporting hooks
 *
 * When the Yandex SDK script is absent (local dev, itch.io, standalone web) every method
 * degrades to a working local stub so the game is 100% playable and testable outside Yandex.
 */
(function (global) {
  'use strict';

  const STORAGE_KEY = 'roy_meta_v1';

  const listeners = {
    pause: [],
    resume: [],
    adOpen: [],
    adClose: [],
    accountDialog: [],
  };

  const state = {
    ysdk: null,
    player: null,
    leaderboards: null,
    ready: false,
    adsAvailable: { fullscreen: false, rewarded: false, banner: false },
    isYandex: false,
    lang: 'ru',
    lastFullscreenAt: 0,
    bannerShown: false,
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * local (non-Yandex) fallback storage
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  const LocalDB = {
    get() {
      try {
        const raw = global.localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
        return raw ? JSON.parse(raw) : null;
      } catch (e) {
        return null;
      }
    },
    set(data) {
      try {
        global.localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(data));
      } catch (e) {
        /* storage disabled -> run in memory only */
      }
    },
  };

  let memoryDB = null;

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * init
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  async function init(opts) {
    opts = opts || {};
    const onLoadingProgress = opts.onLoadingProgress || function () {};

    // The Yandex SDK is injected by the platform into the iframe.
    if (typeof global.YaGames !== 'undefined') {
      state.isYandex = true;
      try {
        state.ysdk = await global.YaGames.init();
        hookPlatformEvents();
        await preloadPlayer();
        await preloadLeaderboards();
        probeAds();
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('[sdk] YaGames.init failed, falling back to local mode', err);
        state.ysdk = null;
      }
    }

    // Tell the platform the game finished loading. Required, otherwise the
    // preloader spinner stays on screen forever.
    try {
      if (state.ysdk && state.ysdk.features && state.ysdk.features.LoadingAPI) {
        state.ysdk.features.LoadingAPI.ready();
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('[sdk] LoadingAPI.ready failed', err);
    }

    try {
      if (state.ysdk && state.ysdk.environment && state.ysdk.environment.i18n) {
        state.lang = state.ysdk.environment.i18n.lang || 'ru';
      }
    } catch (e) {}

    state.ready = true;
    onLoadingProgress(1);
    return state;
  }

  function hookPlatformEvents() {
    const ysdk = state.ysdk;
    if (!ysdk || typeof ysdk.on !== 'function') return;

    // Mandatory: pause gameplay + mute sound while the platform pauses us
    // (ad shown, account dialog, tab hidden).
    try {
      ysdk.on('game_api_pause', () => emit('pause'));
      ysdk.on('game_api_resume', () => emit('resume'));
      ysdk.on('EXIT', () => {
        try { flushCloud(); } catch (e) {}
      });
    } catch (e) {}
  }

  function probeAds() {
    const ysdk = state.ysdk;
    if (!ysdk) return;
    state.adsAvailable.fullscreen = canCall('adv.showFullscreenAdv');
    state.adsAvailable.rewarded = canCall('adv.showRewardedVideo');
    state.adsAvailable.banner = canCall('adv.showBannerAdv');
  }

  function canCall(path) {
    let o = state.ysdk;
    const parts = path.split('.');
    for (const p of parts) {
      if (!o || typeof o[p] === 'undefined') return false;
      o = o[p];
    }
    return typeof o === 'function';
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * gameplay API  (must wrap every ad)
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  function gameplayStart() {
    try {
      if (state.ysdk && state.ysdk.features && state.ysdk.features.GameplayAPI) {
        state.ysdk.features.GameplayAPI.start();
      }
    } catch (e) {}
  }

  function gameplayStop() {
    try {
      if (state.ysdk && state.ysdk.features && state.ysdk.features.GameplayAPI) {
        state.ysdk.features.GameplayAPI.stop();
      }
    } catch (e) {}
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * events
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  function on(name, fn) {
    if (listeners[name]) listeners[name].push(fn);
  }
  function emit(name, payload) {
    (listeners[name] || []).forEach((fn) => {
      try { fn(payload); } catch (e) { console.warn(e); }
    });
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * ads
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */

  /** Interstitial. Yandex forbids showing these more often than ~1 per minute
   *  and never while gameplay is running -> caller must gameplayStop() first. */
  function showInterstitial(opts) {
    opts = opts || {};
    const minGapMs = opts.minGapMs === undefined ? 60000 : opts.minGapMs;
    const now = Date.now();

    if (!state.ysdk || !state.adsAvailable.fullscreen) {
      // Local/dev: simulate a short ad so the flow is still testable.
      return simulateAd('interstitial', opts);
    }
    if (now - state.lastFullscreenAt < minGapMs) {
      if (opts.onClose) opts.onClose();
      return Promise.resolve(false);
    }

    state.lastFullscreenAt = now;
    emit('adOpen');
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      let closed = false;
      const finish = (shown) => {
        if (closed) return;
        closed = true;
        emit('adClose');
        if (opts.onClose) opts.onClose();
        resolve(shown);
      };
      try {
        state.ysdk.adv.showFullscreenAdv({
          callbacks: {
            onOpen: () => emit('adOpen'),
            onClose: (wasShown) => finish(wasShown !== false),
            onError: (err) => { console.warn('[sdk] interstitial error', err); finish(false); },
          },
        });
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('[sdk] interstitial threw', err);
        finish(false);
      }
      // Safety net: some ad providers never fire onClose.
      setTimeout(() => finish(false), 60000);
    });
  }

  /** Rewarded video. Yandex requires the reward to be optional, labelled and
   *  non-essential for progression. UI text is owned by ui.js. */
  function showRewarded(opts) {
    opts = opts || {};
    if (!state.ysdk || !state.adsAvailable.rewarded) {
      return simulateAd('rewarded', opts);
    }

    emit('adOpen');
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      let rewarded = false;
      let closed = false;

      const finish = () => {
        if (closed) return;
        closed = true;
        emit('adClose');
        if (opts.onClose) opts.onClose();
        resolve(rewarded);
      };

      try {
        state.ysdk.adv.showRewardedVideo({
          callbacks: {
            onOpen: () => emit('adOpen'),
            onRewarded: () => {
              rewarded = true;
              if (opts.onReward) opts.onReward();
            },
            onClose: () => finish(),
            onError: (err) => { console.warn('[sdk] rewarded error', err); finish(); },
          },
        });
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('[sdk] rewarded threw', err);
        finish();
      }
      setTimeout(finish, 90000);
    });
  }

  /** Dev-only stand-in so ad-driven UI paths can be exercised locally. */
  function simulateAd(kind, opts) {
    emit('adOpen');
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
      const ms = kind === 'rewarded' ? 1200 : 800;
      setTimeout(() => {
        if (kind === 'rewarded' && opts.onReward) opts.onReward();
        emit('adClose');
        if (opts.onClose) opts.onClose();
        resolve(true);
      }, ms);
    });
  }

  function showBanner() {
    if (state.bannerShown) return;
    if (state.ysdk && state.adsAvailable.banner) {
      try {
        state.ysdk.adv.showBannerAdv();
        state.bannerShown = true;
      } catch (e) { console.warn('[sdk] banner error', e); }
    }
  }

  function hideBanner() {
    if (!state.bannerShown) return;
    if (state.ysdk) {
      try { state.ysdk.adv.hideBannerAdv(); } catch (e) {}
    }
    state.bannerShown = false;
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * player + cloud save
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  async function preloadPlayer() {
    if (!state.ysdk || !canCall('getPlayer')) return;
    try {
      state.player = await state.ysdk.getPlayer({ scopes: false });
    } catch (err) {
      // Player is not authorised -> we keep saving locally. Optional feature.
      state.player = null;
    }
  }

  async function loadData() {
    if (state.player) {
      try {
        const cloud = await state.player.getData(['meta'], false);
        if (cloud && cloud.meta) {
          memoryDB = typeof cloud.meta === 'string' ? JSON.parse(cloud.meta) : cloud.meta;
          LocalDB.set(memoryDB);
          return memoryDB;
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('[sdk] cloud read failed, using local', err);
      }
    }
    memoryDB = LocalDB.get();
    return memoryDB;
  }

  let saveQueued = null;
  function saveData(data) {
    memoryDB = data;
    LocalDB.set(data);
    if (!state.player) return;
    // Debounce cloud writes: Yandex rate-limits setData.
    if (saveQueued) clearTimeout(saveQueued);
    saveQueued = setTimeout(() => { flushCloud(); }, 1500);
  }

  function flushCloud() {
    if (saveQueued) { clearTimeout(saveQueued); saveQueued = null; }
    if (!state.player || !memoryDB) return;
    try {
      state.player.setData({ meta: JSON.stringify(memoryDB) });
    } catch (e) { console.warn('[sdk] cloud write failed', e); }
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * leaderboards
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  async function preloadLeaderboards() {
    if (!state.ysdk || !canCall('getLeaderboards')) return;
    try {
      state.leaderboards = await state.ysdk.getLeaderboards();
    } catch (e) { state.leaderboards = null; }
  }

  async function submitScore(lbName, score) {
    if (!state.leaderboards) return false;
    try {
      await state.leaderboards.setScore(lbName, Math.max(0, Math.floor(score)));
      return true;
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('[sdk] leaderboard submit failed', err);
      return false;
    }
  }

  async function getTopScores(lbName, limit) {
    if (!state.leaderboards) return [];
    try {
      const res = await state.leaderboards.getEntries(lbName, {
        quantityTop: limit || 10,
        includeUser: true,
        quantityAround: 3,
      });
      return (res.entries || []).map((e) => ({
        rank: e.rank,
        score: e.score,
        name: (e.player && (e.player.publicName || e.player.uniqueID)) || '???',
        isMe: !!(e.player && e.player.uniqueID === (state.player && state.player.getUniqueID())),
      }));
    } catch (err) {
      return [];
    }
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * misc
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  function reportEvent(name) {
    try {
      if (state.ysdk && state.ysdk.events && typeof state.ysdk.events.report === 'function') {
        state.ysdk.events.report(name);
      }
    } catch (e) {}
  }

  function getDeviceType() {
    try {
      if (state.ysdk && state.ysdk.deviceInfo) {
        const d = state.ysdk.deviceInfo;
        if (d.isTablet()) return 'tablet';
        if (d.isMobile()) return 'mobile';
        if (d.isTV()) return 'tv';
      }
    } catch (e) {}
    const ua = global.navigator ? global.navigator.userAgent : '';
    if (/Mobi|Android|iPhone|iPad/i.test(ua)) return 'mobile';
    return 'desktop';
  }

  global.SDK = {
    init,
    on,
    gameplayStart,
    gameplayStop,
    showInterstitial,
    showRewarded,
    showBanner,
    hideBanner,
    loadData,
    saveData,
    flushCloud,
    submitScore,
    getTopScores,
    reportEvent,
    getDeviceType,
    get state() { return state; },
  };
})(typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window : globalThis);

/**
 * audio.js — fully procedural WebAudio. Zero asset files = tiny build size.
 * Yandex rule 1.7: game sound must stop during fullscreen ads -> use mute()/unmute().
 */
(function (global) {
  'use strict';

  let ctx = null;
  let master = null;
  let musicGain = null;
  let sfxGain = null;
  let muted = false;
  let musicOn = true;
  let musicNodes = [];
  let started = false;

  function ensure() {
    if (ctx) return ctx;
    const AC = global.AudioContext || global.webkitAudioContext;
    if (!AC) return null;
    ctx = new AC();
    master = ctx.createGain();
    master.gain.value = muted ? 0 : 0.9;
    master.connect(ctx.destination);

    musicGain = ctx.createGain();
    musicGain.gain.value = 0.22;
    musicGain.connect(master);

    sfxGain = ctx.createGain();
    sfxGain.gain.value = 0.55;
    sfxGain.connect(master);
    return ctx;
  }

  function unlock() {
    const c = ensure();
    if (!c) return;
    if (c.state === 'suspended') c.resume();
    if (!started) { started = true; startMusic(); }
  }

  function tone(opts) {
    const c = ensure();
    if (!c || muted) return;
    const {
      type = 'sine', freq = 440, to = null, dur = 0.12, vol = 0.3,
      attack = 0.005, delay = 0, detune = 0, dest = sfxGain,
    } = opts;

    const t0 = c.currentTime + delay;
    const osc = c.createOscillator();
    const g = c.createGain();
    osc.type = type;
    osc.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, t0);
    if (to !== null) osc.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(1, to), t0 + dur);
    if (detune) osc.detune.value = detune;

    g.gain.setValueAtTime(0.0001, t0);
    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(0.0002, vol), t0 + attack);
    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t0 + dur);

    osc.connect(g);
    g.connect(dest);
    osc.start(t0);
    osc.stop(t0 + dur + 0.05);
    return osc;
  }

  function noise(opts) {
    const c = ensure();
    if (!c || muted) return;
    const { dur = 0.18, vol = 0.25, filter = 'lowpass', freq = 900, to = null, delay = 0 } = opts;
    const t0 = c.currentTime + delay;
    const len = Math.floor(c.sampleRate * dur);
    const buf = c.createBuffer(1, len, c.sampleRate);
    const d = buf.getChannelData(0);
    for (let i = 0; i < len; i++) d[i] = (Math.random() * 2 - 1) * (1 - i / len);

    const src = c.createBufferSource();
    src.buffer = buf;
    const bq = c.createBiquadFilter();
    bq.type = filter;
    bq.frequency.setValueAtTime(freq, t0);
    if (to !== null) bq.frequency.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(Math.max(40, to), t0 + dur);
    const g = c.createGain();
    g.gain.setValueAtTime(vol, t0);
    g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001, t0 + dur);
    src.connect(bq); bq.connect(g); g.connect(sfxGain);
    src.start(t0);
  }

  /* ---------------------------- SFX ---------------------------- */
  const SFX = {
    shoot() { tone({ type: 'square', freq: 720, to: 320, dur: 0.06, vol: 0.06 }); },
    orbit() { tone({ type: 'triangle', freq: 300, to: 260, dur: 0.08, vol: 0.05 }); },
    hit() { noise({ dur: 0.05, vol: 0.09, filter: 'highpass', freq: 1800 }); },
    kill() { noise({ dur: 0.12, vol: 0.14, filter: 'lowpass', freq: 1400, to: 300 }); tone({ type: 'sawtooth', freq: 180, to: 60, dur: 0.12, vol: 0.07 }); },
    bigKill() { noise({ dur: 0.3, vol: 0.22, filter: 'lowpass', freq: 900, to: 120 }); tone({ type: 'sawtooth', freq: 120, to: 40, dur: 0.3, vol: 0.12 }); },
    xp() { tone({ type: 'sine', freq: 900 + Math.random() * 200, to: 1500, dur: 0.05, vol: 0.05 }); },
    levelup() {
      [523, 659, 784, 1046].forEach((f, i) =>
        tone({ type: 'triangle', freq: f, dur: 0.28, vol: 0.14, delay: i * 0.07 }));
    },
    evolve() {
      [392, 523, 659, 784, 1046, 1318].forEach((f, i) =>
        tone({ type: 'sawtooth', freq: f, dur: 0.4, vol: 0.1, delay: i * 0.06 }));
      noise({ dur: 0.6, vol: 0.12, filter: 'bandpass', freq: 600, to: 3000 });
    },
    hurt() { tone({ type: 'square', freq: 200, to: 80, dur: 0.2, vol: 0.18 }); noise({ dur: 0.15, vol: 0.15, filter: 'lowpass', freq: 700 }); },
    death() {
      [400, 300, 220, 150, 90].forEach((f, i) =>
        tone({ type: 'sawtooth', freq: f, to: f * 0.5, dur: 0.5, vol: 0.15, delay: i * 0.12 }));
      noise({ dur: 1.0, vol: 0.2, filter: 'lowpass', freq: 800, to: 100 });
    },
    click() { tone({ type: 'square', freq: 500, to: 700, dur: 0.05, vol: 0.08 }); },
    deny() { tone({ type: 'square', freq: 180, to: 120, dur: 0.14, vol: 0.1 }); },
    coin() { tone({ type: 'triangle', freq: 1200, to: 1800, dur: 0.07, vol: 0.1 }); tone({ type: 'triangle', freq: 1800, dur: 0.09, vol: 0.07, delay: 0.05 }); },
    bossWarn() {
      tone({ type: 'sawtooth', freq: 110, to: 90, dur: 1.2, vol: 0.2 });
      tone({ type: 'sawtooth', freq: 112, to: 91, dur: 1.2, vol: 0.16, detune: 15 });
    },
    revive() {
      [261, 329, 392, 523, 659].forEach((f, i) =>
        tone({ type: 'sine', freq: f, dur: 0.6, vol: 0.16, delay: i * 0.09 }));
    },
    chest() {
      noise({ dur: 0.25, vol: 0.14, filter: 'bandpass', freq: 1200, to: 4000 });
      [784, 988, 1175].forEach((f, i) => tone({ type: 'triangle', freq: f, dur: 0.3, vol: 0.11, delay: 0.08 + i * 0.07 }));
    },
  };

  /* ---------------------------- music ---------------------------- */
  // Slow evolving drone + arpeggio. Cheap, loop-free, never gets grating.
  const SCALE = [0, 3, 5, 7, 10, 12, 15];
  let step = 0;
  let musicTimer = null;

  function startMusic() {
    const c = ensure();
    if (!c || !musicOn) return;
    stopMusic();

    // drone pad
    [55, 82.4].forEach((f, i) => {
      const o = c.createOscillator();
      const g = c.createGain();
      const lfo = c.createOscillator();
      const lg = c.createGain();
      o.type = i === 0 ? 'sawtooth' : 'sine';
      o.frequency.value = f;
      g.gain.value = 0.0;
      g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(0.5, c.currentTime + 3);
      lfo.frequency.value = 0.07 + i * 0.05;
      lg.gain.value = 0.25;
      lfo.connect(lg); lg.connect(g.gain);
      const flt = c.createBiquadFilter();
      flt.type = 'lowpass'; flt.frequency.value = 420; flt.Q.value = 3;
      o.connect(flt); flt.connect(g); g.connect(musicGain);
      o.start(); lfo.start();
      musicNodes.push(o, lfo, g, flt, lg);
    });

    musicTimer = setInterval(() => {
      if (muted || !musicOn) return;
      const c2 = ensure();
      if (!c2) return;
      const base = 220;
      const n = SCALE[step % SCALE.length];
      const f = base * Math.pow(2, n / 12);
      if (step % 2 === 0) {
        tone({ type: 'sine', freq: f * 2, dur: 1.6, vol: 0.045, attack: 0.3, dest: musicGain });
      }
      if (step % 8 === 4) {
        tone({ type: 'triangle', freq: f, dur: 2.2, vol: 0.05, attack: 0.5, dest: musicGain });
      }
      step++;
    }, 620);
  }

  function stopMusic() {
    if (musicTimer) { clearInterval(musicTimer); musicTimer = null; }
    musicNodes.forEach((n) => { try { if (n.stop) n.stop(); else n.disconnect(); } catch (e) {} });
    musicNodes = [];
  }

  /* ---------------------------- control ---------------------------- */
  function mute() { muted = true; if (master) master.gain.value = 0; }
  function unmute() { muted = false; if (master) master.gain.value = 0.9; }
  function isMuted() { return muted; }
  function toggleMusic() {
    musicOn = !musicOn;
    if (musicOn) startMusic(); else stopMusic();
    return musicOn;
  }
  function isMusicOn() { return musicOn; }

  global.Audio2 = { unlock, SFX, mute, unmute, isMuted, toggleMusic, isMusicOn, startMusic, stopMusic };
})(typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window : globalThis);

/**
 * config.js — all game balance in one place.
 *
 * RETENTION MODEL (tuned for Yandex Games' ~60 min average playtime):
 *  - First 90 s: 3 level-ups, first evolution teased, first boss warning.
 *  - Level-up cadence starts at ~18 s and eases to ~75 s -> constant "one more level" pull.
 *  - Run cap 15 min with a hard victory at 12 min -> sessions that are long but finishable.
 *  - Every run yields Biomass (meta currency) -> "one more run" even after death.
 */
(function (global) {

   * XP curve: time-to-next-level grows smoothly, never cliffs
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  function xpForLevel(level) {

  }

  const PASSIVES = {

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * ENEMIES
   * hp/dmg scale by time; speed is constant so late game = crowd pressure, not bullet sponge
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  const ENEMY_TYPES = {
    drifter: { hp: 12, speed: 62, dmg: 8, r: 13, xp: 1, color: '#ff6b81', shape: 'blob', biomass: 1 },

    ghost: { hp: 34, speed: 96, dmg: 12, r: 15, xp: 3, color: '#a5b4fc', shape: 'ghost', biomass: 3, phase: true },
    elite: { hp: 520, speed: 66, dmg: 22, r: 32, xp: 40, color: '#ff3d6e', shape: 'elite', biomass: 25, isElite: true, knockResist: 0.85, ranged: true, cd: 1.8, projSpeed: 300 },
    boss: { hp: 3400, speed: 44, dmg: 28, r: 48, xp: 250, color: '#ff1f4b', shape: 'boss', biomass: 150, isBoss: true, knockResist: 1, ranged: true, cd: 1.2, projSpeed: 340, summon: true },
  };

  /**
   * Spawn table. Each entry: [secondFrom, type, weight]
   * Waves get denser and heavier; the curve is the single biggest retention lever.
   */
  const SPAWN_WAVES = [
    { t: 0, types: { drifter: 10 }, perSecond: 1.0, cap: 60 },
    { t: 45, types: { drifter: 8, darter: 4 }, perSecond: 1.5, cap: 90 },
    { t: 90, types: { drifter: 7, darter: 5, splitter: 3 }, perSecond: 2.1, cap: 120 },
    { t: 150, types: { drifter: 6, darter: 5, splitter: 4, spitter: 3 }, perSecond: 2.8, cap: 150 },
    { t: 210, types: { drifter: 5, darter: 5, splitter: 4, spitter: 3, brute: 2 }, perSecond: 3.4, cap: 180 },
    { t: 300, types: { drifter: 4, darter: 4, splitter: 4, spitter: 3, brute: 3, ghost: 2 }, perSecond: 4.1, cap: 210 },
    { t: 400, types: { drifter: 3, darter: 4, splitter: 4, spitter: 4, brute: 3, ghost: 3 }, perSecond: 4.8, cap: 240 },
    { t: 500, types: { drifter: 3, darter: 4, splitter: 3, spitter: 4, brute: 4, ghost: 4 }, perSecond: 5.4, cap: 260 },
    { t: 600, types: { darter: 4, splitter: 4, spitter: 4, brute: 4, ghost: 5 }, perSecond: 6.0, cap: 280 },
    { t: 720, types: { darter: 3, splitter: 4, spitter: 4, brute: 5, ghost: 5 }, perSecond: 6.6, cap: 300 },
  ];

  /** Elite every 3 min, boss at 3 and 6 and 9 min. */
  const TIMED_SPAWNS = [
    { t: 180, type: 'boss' },
    { t: 360, type: 'boss' },
    { t: 540, type: 'boss' },
    { t: 720, type: 'boss' },
  ];
  function eliteAt(t) { return t >= 150 && t % 90 < 1; }

  /** Enemy HP/Damage multiplier over time. */
  function hpScale(t) { return 1 + t / 95 + Math.pow(t / 300, 1.5); }
  function dmgScale(t) { return 1 + t / 240; }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * CHARACTERS  (unlockable -> long-term goal)
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  const CHARACTERS = [
    {
      id: 'larva', name: 'Личинка', color: '#7cf6ff',
      desc: 'Сбалансированный старт. Открывает Иглы.',

      unlock: { type: 'free' },
    },
    {
      id: 'urchin', name: 'Ёж', color: '#b39cff',
      desc: '+40% здоровья, -10% скорости. Открывает Спутников.',
      startWeapon: 'orbit', speedMul: 0.9, hpMul: 1.4, dmgMul: 1.0, xpMul: 1.0,
      unlock: { type: 'score', value: 6000, text: 'Наберите 6 000 очков за забег' },
    },
    {
      id: 'jelly', name: 'Медуза', color: '#ffd166',
      desc: '+25% скорости, -25% здоровья. Открывает Волну.',
      startWeapon: 'nova', speedMul: 1.25, hpMul: 0.75, dmgMul: 1.0, xpMul: 1.0,
      unlock: { type: 'level', value: 20, text: 'Достигните 20 уровня в забеге' },
    },
    {
      id: 'eel', name: 'Угорь', color: '#ff8fab',
      desc: '+20% урона, -15% сбора опыта. Открывает Разряд.',
      startWeapon: 'arc', speedMul: 1.05, hpMul: 0.95, dmgMul: 1.2, xpMul: 0.85,
      unlock: { type: 'evolve', value: 2, text: 'Получите 2 эволюции за один забег' },
    },
    {
      id: 'shrimp', name: 'Креветка', color: '#8bffb0',
      desc: '+30% опыта, но -15% урона. Открывает Мальков.',
      startWeapon: 'swarm', speedMul: 1.1, hpMul: 0.9, dmgMul: 0.85, xpMul: 1.3,
      unlock: { type: 'biomass', value: 1200, text: 'Накопите 1 200 биомассы всего' },
    },
    {
      id: 'anemone', name: 'Анемон', color: '#6ee7ff',
      desc: 'Начинает с 2 оружий: Сеть + Иглы. Медленный, но крепкий.',
      startWeapon: 'trap', bonusWeapon: 'spike', speedMul: 0.88, hpMul: 1.25, dmgMul: 1.05, xpMul: 1.0,
      unlock: { type: 'survive', value: 600, text: 'Выживите 10 минут в забеге' },
    },
  ];

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * META UPGRADES  (the "one more run" engine — spend Biomass)
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  const META = [
    { id: 'm_dmg', name: 'Токсин', desc: '+4% урона ко всем забегам', max: 10, base: 60, growth: 1.55, per: 0.04, icon: 'might', color: '#ff6b6b' },
    { id: 'm_hp', name: 'Хитин', desc: '+8 здоровья', max: 10, base: 50, growth: 1.5, per: 8, icon: 'vigor', color: '#7bff8a' },
    { id: 'm_spd', name: 'Плавники', desc: '+2% скорости', max: 8, base: 55, growth: 1.5, per: 0.02, icon: 'haste', color: '#4ecdc4' },
    { id: 'm_cd', name: 'Нервы', desc: '-2% перезарядки', max: 8, base: 65, growth: 1.55, per: 0.02, icon: 'focus', color: '#ffd93d' },
    { id: 'm_mag', name: 'Усики', desc: '+10% радиуса сбора', max: 6, base: 45, growth: 1.45, per: 0.10, icon: 'magnet', color: '#c3a6ff' },
    { id: 'm_xp', name: 'Метаболизм', desc: '+5% получаемого опыта', max: 8, base: 70, growth: 1.6, per: 0.05, icon: 'regen', color: '#ff9ff3' },
    { id: 'm_rev', name: 'Второе дыхание', desc: '+1 бесплатное воскрешение за забег', max: 3, base: 400, growth: 2.4, per: 1, icon: 'heart', color: '#ff4d6d' },
    { id: 'm_luck', name: 'Удача', desc: '+8% шанс редкого варианта при выборе', max: 5, base: 150, growth: 1.7, per: 0.08, icon: 'star', color: '#ffe066' },
  ];

  function metaCost(item, level) {
    return Math.floor(item.base * Math.pow(item.growth, level));
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * Player base stats
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  const PLAYER = {
    hp: 120,
    speed: 178,
    pickup: 62,
    r: 15,
    iFrames: 0.6,
  };

  global.CFG = {
    RUN_MINUTES,
    VICTORY_SECOND,
    xpForLevel,
    WEAPONS,
    PASSIVES,
    EVOLUTIONS,
    ENEMY_TYPES,
    SPAWN_WAVES,
    TIMED_SPAWNS,
    eliteAt,
    hpScale,
    dmgScale,
    CHARACTERS,
    META,
    metaCost,
    PLAYER,
  };

input.content:
/**
 * engine.js — game state + simulation. Pure logic, no DOM.
 * The renderer and UI read from this; the loop calls Game.update(dt).
 */
(function (global) {
  'use strict';

  const CFG = global.CFG;

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * small utils
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  const rand = (a, b) => a + Math.random() * (b - a);
  const randInt = (a, b) => Math.floor(rand(a, b + 1));
  const pick = (arr) => arr[Math.floor(Math.random() * arr.length)];
  const clamp = (v, a, b) => (v < a ? a : v > b ? b : v);
  const lerp = (a, b, t) => a + (b - a) * t;
  const TAU = Math.PI * 2;

  function weightedPick(weights) {
    let total = 0;
    for (const k in weights) total += weights[k];
    let r = Math.random() * total;
    for (const k in weights) {
      r -= weights[k];
      if (r <= 0) return k;
    }
    return Object.keys(weights)[0];
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * persistent meta (biomass, upgrades, unlocks, best run)
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  const DEFAULT_META = {
    v: 1,
    biomass: 0,
    totalBiomass: 0,
    upgrades: {},
    unlocked: { larva: true },
    bestScore: 0,
    bestTime: 0,
    bestLevel: 0,
    runs: 0,
    kills: 0,
    evolutions: 0,
    bosses: 0,
    lastSeen: 0,
    daily: { day: '', bonusUsed: false },
  };

  function freshMeta() {
    const m = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(DEFAULT_META));
    m.lastSeen = Date.now();
    return m;
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * Game
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  const Game = {
    meta: null,
    state: 'boot', // boot | menu | meta | playing | levelup | paused | gameover | victory
    character: null,
    charId: 'larva',
    t: 0,             // run seconds
    frame: 0,
    score: 0,
    level: 1,
    xp: 0,
    xpNeed: 1,
    kills: 0,
    runBiomass: 0,
    reviveUsed: 0,
    reviveFree: 0,
    bossKills: 0,
    evolutionsRun: 0,
    victory: false,
    shake: 0,
    hitstop: 0,
    flash: 0,
    banner: null,
    bannerT: 0,

    player: null,
    enemies: [],
    bullets: [],
    ebullets: [],
    gems: [],
    particles: [],
    orbiters: [],
    novae: [],
    traps: [],
    floaters: [],
    chestDrops: [],

    weapons: {},   // id -> {level, timer, evolved}
    passives: {},  // id -> level
    offerQueue: 0, // pending level-up choices
    offers: [],

    view: { w: 960, h: 600, camX: 0, camY: 0, dpr: 1 },
    input: { x: 0, y: 0, active: false, kx: 0, ky: 0 },

    onEvent: null, // ui.js subscribes
  };

  function emit(name, data) {
    if (Game.onEvent) {
      try { Game.onEvent(name, data); } catch (e) { console.warn(e); }
    }
  }
  Game.emit = emit;

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * derived stats
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  function passiveLevel(id) { return Game.passives[id] || 0; }

  function metaBonus(id) {
    const lvl = Game.meta.upgrades[id] || 0;
    const item = CFG.META.find((m) => m.id === id);
    return item ? lvl * item.per : 0;
  }

  function stats() {
    const ch = Game.character;
    const dmgMul = (1 + passiveLevel('might') * CFG.PASSIVES.might.per) *
      ch.dmgMul * (1 + metaBonus('m_dmg'));
    const cdMul = Math.max(0.35, (1 - passiveLevel('focus') * CFG.PASSIVES.focus.per) *
      (1 - metaBonus('m_cd')) * ch.speedMul);
    const speed = CFG.PLAYER.speed *
      (1 + passiveLevel('haste') * CFG.PASSIVES.haste.per) *
      ch.speedMul * (1 + metaBonus('m_spd'));
    const maxHp = CFG.PLAYER.hp +
      passiveLevel('vigor') * CFG.PASSIVES.vigor.per +
      metaBonus('m_hp') +
      (ch.hpMul - 1) * CFG.PLAYER.hp;
    const pickup = CFG.PLAYER.pickup *
      (1 + passiveLevel('magnet') * CFG.PASSIVES.magnet.per) *
      (1 + metaBonus('m_mag'));
    const regen = passiveLevel('regen') * CFG.PASSIVES.regen.per;
    const xpMul = ch.xpMul * (1 + metaBonus('m_xp'));
    const luck = 1 + metaBonus('m_luck');
    return { dmgMul, cdMul, speed, maxHp, pickup, regen, xpMul, luck };
  }
  Game.stats = stats;

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * run lifecycle
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  Game.newRun = function (charId) {
    Game.charId = charId || Game.charId;
    const ch = CFG.CHARACTERS.find((c) => c.id === Game.charId) || CFG.CHARACTERS[0];
    Game.character = ch;

    const st = stats();
    Game.t = 0; Game.frame = 0; Game.score = 0; Game.level = 1; Game.xp = 0;
    Game.xpNeed = CFG.xpForLevel(1); Game.kills = 0; Game.runBiomass = 0;
    Game.reviveUsed = 0; Game.reviveFree = Game.meta.upgrades.m_rev || 0;
    Game.bossKills = 0; Game.evolutionsRun = 0; Game.victory = false;
    Game.shake = 0; Game.hitstop = 0; Game.flash = 0; Game.banner = null; Game.bannerT = 0;

    Game.enemies.length = 0; Game.bullets.length = 0; Game.ebullets.length = 0;
    Game.gems.length = 0; Game.particles.length = 0; Game.orbiters.length = 0;
    Game.novae.length = 0; Game.traps.length = 0; Game.floaters.length = 0;
    Game.chestDrops.length = 0;
    Game.weapons = {}; Game.passives = {}; Game.offerQueue = 0; Game.offers = [];

    Game.player = {
      x: 0, y: 0, vx: 0, vy: 0,
      hp: st.maxHp, maxHp: st.maxHp,
      r: CFG.PLAYER.r, iFrame: 0, facing: 1,
      trail: [],
    };

    addWeapon(ch.startWeapon);
    if (ch.bonusWeapon) addWeapon(ch.bonusWeapon);

    Game.state = 'playing';
    Game.spawnAcc = 0;
    Game.timedFired = {};
    Game.eliteAcc = 0;
    emit('runStart');
  };

  function addWeapon(id) {
    if (Game.weapons[id]) return;
    Game.weapons[id] = { level: 1, timer: 0.6, evolved: false };
    Game.orbiters.length = 0;
    emit('weaponChange');
  }

  function levelWeapon(id) {
    const w = Game.weapons[id];
    if (!w) { addWeapon(id); return; }
    const def = CFG.WEAPONS[id];
    if (w.level < def.maxLevel) { w.level++; w.evolved = false; }
    Game.orbiters.length = 0;
    emit('weaponChange');
  }

  function levelPassive(id) {
    const def = CFG.PASSIVES[id];
    Game.passives[id] = (Game.passives[id] || 0) + 1;
    if (Game.passives[id] > def.maxLevel) Game.passives[id] = def.maxLevel;
    const st = stats();
    Game.player.maxHp = st.maxHp;
    if (id === 'vigor') heal(22);
    emit('weaponChange');
  }

  function heal(amount) {
    const p = Game.player;
    p.hp = Math.min(p.maxHp, p.hp + amount);
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * level-up offers
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  function canEvolve(weaponId) {
    const ev = CFG.EVOLUTIONS[weaponId];
    if (!ev) return false;
    const w = Game.weapons[weaponId];
    if (!w || w.evolved) return false;
    const r = ev.requires;
    if (w.level < r.weaponLevel) return false;
    if (passiveLevel(r.passive) < r.passiveLevel) return false;
    return true;
  }

  function buildOffers() {
    const st = stats();
    const pool = [];

    // Evolutions always win the roll — they are the payoff moment.
    for (const wid of Object.keys(Game.weapons)) {
      if (canEvolve(wid)) {
        const ev = CFG.EVOLUTIONS[wid];
        pool.push({
          kind: 'evolution', id: wid, rare: true,
          name: ev.result.name, desc: 'ЭВОЛЮЦИЯ: ' + CFG.WEAPONS[wid].name + ' → ' + ev.result.name,
          color: ev.result.color, icon: ev.result.icon,
        });
      }
    }

    // New weapons
    const ownedW = Object.keys(Game.weapons);
    for (const wid of Object.keys(CFG.WEAPONS)) {
      if (ownedW.includes(wid)) continue;
      if (ownedW.length >= 6) break;
      pool.push({
        kind: 'newWeapon', id: wid, rare: false,
        name: CFG.WEAPONS[wid].name, desc: 'Новое оружие: ' + CFG.WEAPONS[wid].desc,
        color: CFG.WEAPONS[wid].color, icon: CFG.WEAPONS[wid].icon,
      });
    }

    // Weapon levels
    for (const wid of ownedW) {
      const w = Game.weapons[wid];
      const def = CFG.WEAPONS[wid];
      if (w.evolved) continue;
      if (w.level >= def.maxLevel) continue;
      pool.push({
        kind: 'weaponLevel', id: wid, rare: w.level >= def.maxLevel - 1,
        name: def.name + ' ур.' + (w.level + 1),
        desc: 'Улучшить ' + def.name + ' до уровня ' + (w.level + 1),
        color: def.color, icon: def.icon,
      });
    }

    // Passives
    for (const pid of Object.keys(CFG.PASSIVES)) {
      const def = CFG.PASSIVES[pid];
      const lvl = passiveLevel(pid);
      if (lvl >= def.maxLevel) continue;
      pool.push({
        kind: 'passive', id: pid, rare: lvl === def.maxLevel - 1,
        name: def.name + ' ур.' + (lvl + 1), desc: def.desc,
        color: def.color, icon: def.icon,
      });
    }

    // Fallback: heal
    if (pool.length === 0 || Math.random() < 0.12) {
      pool.push({ kind: 'heal', id: 'heal', rare: false, name: 'Восстановление', desc: '+45 здоровья', color: '#7bff8a', icon: 'heart' });
    }

    // pick 3, biasing toward rare with luck
    const out = [];
    const src = pool.slice();
    while (out.length < 3 && src.length) {
      let idx;
      const rares = src.filter((p) => p.rare);
      if (rares.length && Math.random() < 0.34 * st.luck) {
        idx = src.indexOf(pick(rares));
      } else {
        idx = randInt(0, src.length - 1);
      }
      out.push(src.splice(idx, 1)[0]);
    }
    return out;
  }

  function addXp(amount) {
    Game.xp += amount * stats().xpMul;
    while (Game.xp >= Game.xpNeed) {
      Game.xp -= Game.xpNeed;
      Game.level++;
      Game.xpNeed = CFG.xpForLevel(Game.level);
      Game.offerQueue++;
      global.Audio2 && global.Audio2.SFX.levelup();
      burst(Game.player.x, Game.player.y, 26, '#ffe066', 260);
      emit('levelUp', { level: Game.level });
    }
    if (Game.offerQueue > 0 && Game.state === 'playing') {
      Game.offers = buildOffers();
      Game.state = 'levelup';
      Game.offerQueue--;
      emit('levelUpOffer', Game.offers);
    }
  }

  Game.chooseOffer = function (index) {
    const o = Game.offers[index];
    if (!o) return;
    switch (o.kind) {
      case 'newWeapon': addWeapon(o.id); break;
      case 'weaponLevel': levelWeapon(o.id); break;
      case 'passive': levelPassive(o.id); break;
      case 'heal': heal(45); break;
      case 'evolution': {
        const ev = CFG.EVOLUTIONS[o.id];
        Game.weapons[o.id].evolved = true;
        Game.weapons[o.id].level = CFG.WEAPONS[o.id].maxLevel;
        Game.orbiters.length = 0;
        Game.evolutionsRun++;
        Game.score += 250;
        global.Audio2 && global.Audio2.SFX.evolve();
        Game.shake = 16; Game.flash = 0.5;
        burst(Game.player.x, Game.player.y, 60, ev.result.color, 420);
        showBanner(ev.result.name + '!', ev.result.color);
        checkUnlocks();
        emit('weaponChange');
        break;
      }
    }
    Game.offers = [];
    Game.state = Game.offerQueue > 0 ? 'playing' : 'playing';
    if (Game.offerQueue > 0) {
      Game.offers = buildOffers();
      Game.state = 'levelup';
      Game.offerQueue--;
      emit('levelUpOffer', Game.offers);
    }
    global.Audio2 && global.Audio2.SFX.click();
  };

  function showBanner(text, color) {
    Game.banner = { text, color: color || '#fff' };
    Game.bannerT = 2.4;
    emit('banner', Game.banner);
  }
  Game.showBanner = showBanner;

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * particles / floaters
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  function burst(x, y, n, color, speed) {
    if (Game.particles.length > 900) return;
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      const a = Math.random() * TAU;
      const s = rand(speed * 0.25, speed);
      Game.particles.push({
        x, y, vx: Math.cos(a) * s, vy: Math.sin(a) * s,
        life: rand(0.25, 0.7), max: 0.7, color, r: rand(1.5, 4),
      });
    }
  }
  Game.burst = burst;

  function floater(x, y, text, color) {
    if (Game.floaters.length > 60) return;
    Game.floaters.push({ x, y, text, color, life: 0.8 });
  }
  Game.floater = floater;

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * spawning
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  Game.spawnAcc = 0;
  Game.timedFired = {};
  Game.eliteAcc = 0;

  function currentWave(t) {
    let w = CFG.SPAWN_WAVES[0];
    for (const s of CFG.SPAWN_WAVES) if (t >= s.t) w = s;
    return w;
  }

  function spawnEnemy(type, x, y) {
    const def = CFG.ENEMY_TYPES[type];
    if (!def) return null;
    const t = Game.t;
    const hs = CFG.hpScale(t);
    const ds = CFG.dmgScale(t);
    const e = {
      type, def,
      x, y, vx: 0, vy: 0,
      hp: def.hp * hs * (def.isBoss ? 1 + Game.bossKills * 0.35 : 1),
      maxHp: def.hp * hs * (def.isBoss ? 1 + Game.bossKills * 0.35 : 1),
      dmg: def.dmg * ds,
      r: def.r, speed: def.speed,
      xp: def.xp, color: def.color,
      knockX: 0, knockY: 0, slowT: 0, slowAmt: 0,
      hitFlash: 0, cd: rand(0.4, 1.6), wobbleA: Math.random() * TAU,
      spawnT: 0, summonT: 4,
    };
    Game.enemies.push(e);
    return e;
  }

  function spawnAroundPlayer(type, dist) {
    const a = Math.random() * TAU;
    const d = dist || rand(0.62, 1.05) * Math.max(Game.view.w, Game.view.h) * 0.62;
    return spawnEnemy(type, Game.player.x + Math.cos(a) * d, Game.player.y + Math.sin(a) * d);
  }

  function spawnWave(dt) {
    const w = currentWave(Game.t);
    Game.spawnAcc += w.perSecond * dt;
    while (Game.spawnAcc >= 1) {
      Game.spawnAcc -= 1;
      if (Game.enemies.length >= w.cap) break;
      spawnAroundPlayer(weightedPick(w.types));
    }

    // elites
    if (Game.t >= 150) {
      Game.eliteAcc += dt;
      if (Game.eliteAcc >= 60) {
        Game.eliteAcc = 0;
        const e = spawnAroundPlayer('elite');
        if (e) { showBanner('ЭЛИТА!', '#ff3d6e'); Game.shake = 10; global.Audio2 && global.Audio2.SFX.bossWarn(); }
      }
    }

    // bosses
    for (const ts of CFG.TIMED_SPAWNS) {
      if (Game.t >= ts.t && !Game.timedFired[ts.t]) {
        Game.timedFired[ts.t] = true;
        const b = spawnAroundPlayer('boss', 700);
        if (b) {
          showBanner('⚠ БОСС ПРИБЛИЖАЕТСЯ ⚠', '#ff1f4b');
          Game.shake = 20;
          global.Audio2 && global.Audio2.SFX.bossWarn();
        }
      }
    }
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * combat helpers
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  function nearestEnemy(x, y, maxDist) {
    let best = null, bd = maxDist ? maxDist * maxDist : Infinity;
    for (let i = 0; i < Game.enemies.length; i++) {
      const e = Game.enemies[i];
      const dx = e.x - x, dy = e.y - y;
      const d = dx * dx + dy * dy;
      if (d < bd) { bd = d; best = e; }
    }
    return best;
  }

  function damageEnemy(e, amount, opts) {
    if (e.dead) return;
    opts = opts || {};
    const st = stats();
    const dmg = amount * st.dmgMul;
    e.hp -= dmg;
    e.hitFlash = 0.12;
    if (opts.knock) {
      const res = e.def.knockResist || 0;
      const a = opts.angle !== undefined ? opts.angle : Math.atan2(e.y - Game.player.y, e.x - Game.player.x);
      e.knockX += Math.cos(a) * opts.knock * (1 - res);
      e.knockY += Math.sin(a) * opts.knock * (1 - res);
    }
    if (opts.slow) { e.slowAmt = Math.max(e.slowAmt, opts.slow); e.slowT = Math.max(e.slowT, opts.slowTime || 1); }
    if (opts.showDmg && dmg >= 12) floater(e.x + rand(-8, 8), e.y - e.r, Math.round(dmg), '#ffffff');

    if (e.hp <= 0) killEnemy(e);
  }
  Game.damageEnemy = damageEnemy;

  function killEnemy(e) {
    if (e.dead) return;
    e.dead = true;
    Game.kills++;
    Game.meta.kills++;
    Game.runBiomass += e.def.biomass;
    Game.score += Math.round(e.maxHp * 0.6 + e.def.biomass * 8);
    const big = e.def.isBoss || e.def.isElite;
    burst(e.x, e.y, big ? 60 : Math.round(6 + e.r * 0.5), e.color, big ? 420 : 200);
    if (big) {
      Game.shake = big && e.def.isBoss ? 26 : 14;
      Game.hitstop = e.def.isBoss ? 0.22 : 0.1;
      global.Audio2 && global.Audio2.SFX.bigKill();
      if (e.def.isBoss) {
        Game.bossKills++;
        Game.meta.bosses++;
        Game.score += 1500;
        showBanner('БОСС ПОВЕРЖЕН! +1500', '#ffe066');
        dropChest(e.x, e.y, 3);
      } else {
        dropChest(e.x, e.y, 1);
      }
    } else {
      global.Audio2 && global.Audio2.SFX.kill();
    }

    // splitter
    if (e.def.splits && !e.noSplit) {
      for (let i = 0; i < e.def.splits; i++) {
        const a = Math.random() * TAU;
        const child = spawnEnemy('drifter', e.x + Math.cos(a) * 12, e.y + Math.sin(a) * 12);
        if (child) {
          child.noSplit = true;
          child.hp *= 0.9; child.maxHp = child.hp;
          child.speed *= 1.35; child.r *= 0.75;
          child.color = '#d8a6ff';
        }
      }
    }

    // xp gems
    const n = e.def.isBoss ? 24 : e.def.isElite ? 8 : 1;
    for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
      const a = Math.random() * TAU;
      const d = e.def.isBoss ? rand(10, 60) : rand(0, 10);
      Game.gems.push({
        x: e.x + Math.cos(a) * d, y: e.y + Math.sin(a) * d,
        vx: Math.cos(a) * 40, vy: Math.sin(a) * 40,
        v: Math.max(1, Math.round(e.xp / n)), r: e.def.isBoss ? 7 : 4.5,
        pull: 0, life: 0,
      });
    }
  }

  function dropChest(x, y, count) {
    for (let i = 0; i < count; i++) {
      Game.chestDrops.push({ x: x + rand(-30, 30), y: y + rand(-30, 30), r: 13, life: 22, pulse: 0 });
    }
    global.Audio2 && global.Audio2.SFX.chest();
  }

  function hurtPlayer(amount, src) {
    const p = Game.player;
    if (p.iFrame > 0 || Game.state !== 'playing') return;
    p.hp -= amount;
    p.iFrame = CFG.PLAYER.iFrames;
    Game.shake = Math.min(24, 8 + amount * 0.5);
    Game.flash = 0.35;
    global.Audio2 && global.Audio2.SFX.hurt();
    floater(p.x, p.y - 24, '-' + Math.round(amount), '#ff4d6d');
    if (src) {
      const a = Math.atan2(p.y - src.y, p.x - src.x);
      p.vx += Math.cos(a) * 140; p.vy += Math.sin(a) * 140;
    }
    if (p.hp <= 0) { p.hp = 0; onDeath(); }
  }

  function onDeath() {
    Game.state = 'gameover';
    Game.shake = 30;
    Game.flash = 0.8;
    burst(Game.player.x, Game.player.y, 80, Game.character.color, 500);
    global.Audio2 && global.Audio2.SFX.death();
    finishRun(false);
    emit('gameOver');
  }

  Game.revive = function (free) {
    const p = Game.player;
    p.hp = p.maxHp * 0.6;
    p.iFrame = 2.5;
    Game.state = 'playing';
    Game.reviveUsed++;
    Game.flash = 0.6;
    Game.shake = 18;
    burst(p.x, p.y, 70, '#7bff8a', 420);
    global.Audio2 && global.Audio2.SFX.revive();
    showBanner('ВОСКРЕШЕНИЕ!', '#7bff8a');
    emit('revive', { free });
  };

  function finishRun(won) {
    const bm = Math.round(Game.runBiomass + Game.score / 40 + Game.level * 4 + (won ? 300 : 0));
    Game.runBiomassResult = bm;
    Game.meta.biomass += bm;
    Game.meta.totalBiomass += bm;
    Game.meta.runs++;
    Game.meta.evolutions += Game.evolutionsRun;
    if (Game.score > Game.meta.bestScore) Game.meta.bestScore = Game.score;
    if (Game.t > Game.meta.bestTime) Game.meta.bestTime = Game.t;
    if (Game.level > Game.meta.bestLevel) Game.meta.bestLevel = Game.level;
    Game.victory = won;
    checkUnlocks();
    global.SDK.saveData(Game.meta);
    global.SDK.submitScore('roy_best', Game.score);
    emit('runEnd', { biomass: bm, won });
  }

  Game.claimVictory = function () {
    if (Game.state !== 'victory') return;
    Game.state = 'playing';
    Game.victoryScored = true;
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * unlocks
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  function checkUnlocks() {
    const m = Game.meta;
    let unlockedAny = null;
    for (const c of CFG.CHARACTERS) {
      if (m.unlocked[c.id]) continue;
      let ok = false;
      switch (c.unlock.type) {
        case 'free': ok = true; break;
        case 'score': ok = m.bestScore >= c.unlock.value; break;
        case 'level': ok = m.bestLevel >= c.unlock.value; break;
        case 'evolve': ok = Game.evolutionsRun >= c.unlock.value; break;
        case 'biomass': ok = m.totalBiomass >= c.unlock.value; break;
        case 'survive': ok = m.bestTime >= c.unlock.value; break;
      }
      if (ok) { m.unlocked[c.id] = true; unlockedAny = c; }
    }
    if (unlockedAny) {
      showBanner('НОВЫЙ ГЕРОЙ: ' + unlockedAny.name.toUpperCase(), unlockedAny.color);
      global.Audio2 && global.Audio2.SFX.chest();
      emit('unlock', unlockedAny);
    }
  }
  Game.checkUnlocks = checkUnlocks;

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * weapon firing
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  function fireWeapons(dt) {
    const p = Game.player;
    const st = stats();

    for (const id of Object.keys(Game.weapons)) {
      const w = Game.weapons[id];

      if (w.evolved) { fireEvolved(id, dt, st); continue; }

      const def = CFG.WEAPONS[id];
      const L = def.levels[w.level - 1];

      switch (id) {
        case 'spike': {
          w.timer -= dt;
          if (w.timer <= 0) {
            w.timer = L.cd * st.cdMul;
            const target = nearestEnemy(p.x, p.y, 620);
            const base = target ? Math.atan2(target.y - p.y, target.x - p.x) : (p.vx || p.vy ? Math.atan2(p.vy, p.vx) : 0);
            const spread = 0.16 * (L.count - 1);
            for (let i = 0; i < L.count; i++) {
              const a = base + (L.count === 1 ? 0 : -spread / 2 + (spread * i) / (L.count - 1));
              Game.bullets.push({
                x: p.x, y: p.y, vx: Math.cos(a) * L.speed, vy: Math.sin(a) * L.speed,
                dmg: L.dmg, pierce: L.pierce, r: 5, life: 1.6, color: def.color, hit: new Set(), kind: 'spike',
              });
            }
            global.Audio2 && global.Audio2.SFX.shoot();
          }
          break;
        }
        case 'orbit': {
          // orbiters are rebuilt when count changes
          if (Game.orbiters.length !== L.count || Game.orbiters[0].w !== id) {
            Game.orbiters.length = 0;
            for (let i = 0; i < L.count; i++) {
              Game.orbiters.push({ w: id, a: (TAU * i) / L.count, hitCd: 0 });
            }
          }
          for (const o of Game.orbiters) {
            o.a += L.spin * dt;
            o.hitCd -= dt;
            o.x = p.x + Math.cos(o.a) * L.radius;
            o.y = p.y + Math.sin(o.a) * L.radius;
            for (let i = 0; i < Game.enemies.length; i++) {
              const e = Game.enemies[i];
              const dx = e.x - o.x, dy = e.y - o.y;
              if (dx * dx + dy * dy < (e.r + L.size) * (e.r + L.size) && o.hitCd <= 0) {
                o.hitCd = 0.3;
                damageEnemy(e, L.dmg, { knock: L.knock, angle: o.a, showDmg: true });
                burst(o.x, o.y, 5, def.color, 150);
              }
            }
          }
          break;
        }
        case 'nova': {
          w.timer -= dt;
          if (w.timer <= 0) {
            w.timer = L.cd * st.cdMul;
            Game.novae.push({ x: p.x, y: p.y, r: 20, maxR: L.radius, life: 0.45, max: 0.45, dmg: L.dmg, knock: L.knock, slow: L.slow, slowTime: L.slowTime, color: def.color, hit: new Set() });
            global.Audio2 && global.Audio2.SFX.orbit();
            Game.shake = Math.max(Game.shake, 5);
          }
          break;
        }
        case 'arc': {
          w.timer -= dt;
          if (w.timer <= 0) {
            w.timer = L.cd * st.cdMul;
            let from = { x: p.x, y: p.y };
            const used = new Set();
            let cur = nearestEnemy(from.x, from.y, L.range);
            const pts = [{ x: p.x, y: p.y }];
            for (let j = 0; j < L.jumps && cur; j++) {
              damageEnemy(cur, L.dmg * Math.pow(0.92, j), { showDmg: j === 0 });
              burst(cur.x, cur.y, 6, def.color, 130);
              pts.push({ x: cur.x, y: cur.y });
              used.add(cur);
              let nxt = null, nd = L.range * L.range;
              for (let i = 0; i < Game.enemies.length; i++) {
                const e = Game.enemies[i];
                if (used.has(e) || e.dead) continue;
                const dx = e.x - cur.x, dy = e.y - cur.y;
                const d = dx * dx + dy * dy;
                if (d < nd) { nd = d; nxt = e; }
              }
              cur = nxt;
            }
            if (pts.length > 1) {
              Game.novae.push({ bolt: pts, life: 0.22, max: 0.22, color: def.color });
              global.Audio2 && global.Audio2.SFX.shoot();
            }
          }
          break;
        }
        case 'swarm': {
          w.timer -= dt;
          if (w.timer <= 0) {
            w.timer = L.cd * st.cdMul;
            for (let i = 0; i < L.count; i++) {
              const a = Math.random() * TAU;
              Game.bullets.push({
                x: p.x + Math.cos(a) * 12, y: p.y + Math.sin(a) * 12,
                vx: Math.cos(a) * L.speed, vy: Math.sin(a) * L.speed,
                dmg: L.dmg, pierce: 0, r: 6, life: L.life, color: def.color,
                hit: new Set(), kind: 'swarm', homing: L.homing, speed: L.speed, wob: Math.random() * TAU,
              });
            }
          }
          break;
        }
        case 'trap': {
          w.timer -= dt;
          if (w.timer <= 0) {
            w.timer = L.cd * st.cdMul;
            for (let i = 0; i < L.count; i++) {
              const a = Math.random() * TAU;
              const d = rand(30, 130);
              Game.traps.push({
                x: p.x + Math.cos(a) * d, y: p.y + Math.sin(a) * d,
                r: L.radius, life: L.life, dmg: L.dmg, tick: L.tick, tickT: 0, color: def.color, pulse: 0,
              });
            }
            Game.traps = Game.traps.slice(-40);
          }
          break;
        }
      }
    }
  }

  function fireEvolved(id, dt, st) {
    const p = Game.player;
    const def = CFG.EVOLUTIONS[id].result;
    const w = Game.weapons[id];
    w.timer -= dt;

    if (id === 'spike') {
      if (w.timer <= 0) {
        w.timer = def.cd * st.cdMul;
        const target = nearestEnemy(p.x, p.y, 700);
        const base = target ? Math.atan2(target.y - p.y, target.x - p.x) : Math.random() * TAU;
        for (let i = 0; i < def.count; i++) {
          const a = base - def.spread / 2 + (def.spread * i) / (def.count - 1);
          Game.bullets.push({
            x: p.x, y: p.y, vx: Math.cos(a) * def.speed, vy: Math.sin(a) * def.speed,
            dmg: def.dmg, pierce: def.pierce, r: 7, life: 1.8, color: def.color,
            hit: new Set(), kind: 'spike', homing: def.homing, speed: def.speed,
          });
        }
        global.Audio2 && global.Audio2.SFX.shoot();
      }
    } else if (id === 'orbit') {
      if (Game.orbiters.length !== def.count || Game.orbiters[0].w !== id) {
        Game.orbiters.length = 0;
        for (let i = 0; i < def.count; i++) Game.orbiters.push({ w: id, a: (TAU * i) / def.count, hitCd: 0 });
      }
      for (const o of Game.orbiters) {
        o.a += def.spin * dt;
        o.hitCd -= dt;
        o.x = p.x + Math.cos(o.a) * def.radius;
        o.y = p.y + Math.sin(o.a) * def.radius;
        for (let i = 0; i < Game.enemies.length; i++) {
          const e = Game.enemies[i];
          const dx = e.x - o.x, dy = e.y - o.y;
          if (dx * dx + dy * dy < (e.r + def.size) * (e.r + def.size) && o.hitCd <= 0) {
            o.hitCd = 0.22;
            damageEnemy(e, def.dmg, { knock: def.knock, angle: o.a, showDmg: true });
            burst(o.x, o.y, 8, def.color, 200);
          }
        }
      }
    } else if (id === 'nova') {
      if (w.timer <= 0) {
        w.timer = def.cd * st.cdMul;
        Game.novae.push({ x: p.x, y: p.y, r: 30, maxR: def.radius, life: 0.55, max: 0.55, dmg: def.dmg, knock: def.knock, slow: def.slow, slowTime: def.slowTime, color: def.color, hit: new Set(), chain: def.chain });
        Game.shake = Math.max(Game.shake, 8);
        global.Audio2 && global.Audio2.SFX.orbit();
      }
    } else if (id === 'arc') {
      if (w.timer <= 0) {
        w.timer = def.cd * st.cdMul;
        const used = new Set();
        let cur = nearestEnemy(p.x, p.y, def.range);
        const pts = [{ x: p.x, y: p.y }];
        for (let j = 0; j < def.jumps && cur; j++) {
          damageEnemy(cur, def.dmg * Math.pow(0.94, j), { showDmg: j < 2 });
          burst(cur.x, cur.y, 7, def.color, 160);
          pts.push({ x: cur.x, y: cur.y });
          used.add(cur);
          let nxt = null, nd = def.range * def.range;
          for (let i = 0; i < Game.enemies.length; i++) {
            const e = Game.enemies[i];
            if (used.has(e) || e.dead) continue;
            const dx = e.x - cur.x, dy = e.y - cur.y;
            const d = dx * dx + dy * dy;
            if (d < nd) { nd = d; nxt = e; }
          }
          cur = nxt;
        }
        if (pts.length > 1) {
          Game.novae.push({ bolt: pts, life: 0.28, max: 0.28, color: def.color });
          global.Audio2 && global.Audio2.SFX.shoot();
        }
      }
    } else if (id === 'swarm') {
      if (w.timer <= 0) {
        w.timer = def.cd * st.cdMul;
        for (let i = 0; i < def.count; i++) {
          const a = Math.random() * TAU;
          Game.bullets.push({
            x: p.x + Math.cos(a) * 14, y: p.y + Math.sin(a) * 14,
            vx: Math.cos(a) * def.speed, vy: Math.sin(a) * def.speed,
            dmg: def.dmg, pierce: 1, r: 8, life: def.life, color: def.color,
            hit: new Set(), kind: 'swarm', homing: def.homing, speed: def.speed, wob: Math.random() * TAU,
          });
        }
      }
    } else if (id === 'trap') {
      if (w.timer <= 0) {
        w.timer = def.cd * st.cdMul;
        for (let i = 0; i < def.count; i++) {
          const a = (TAU * i) / def.count + Game.t;
          const d = rand(40, 150);
          Game.traps.push({
            x: p.x + Math.cos(a) * d, y: p.y + Math.sin(a) * d,
            r: def.radius, life: def.life, dmg: def.dmg, tick: def.tick, tickT: 0, color: def.color, pulse: 0,
          });
        }
        Game.traps = Game.traps.slice(-60);
      }
    }
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * main update
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  Game.update = function (dtRaw) {
    if (Game.state !== 'playing') return;

    // hitstop = tiny freeze on big kills, huge game-feel payoff
    if (Game.hitstop > 0) { Game.hitstop -= dtRaw; return; }

    const dt = Math.min(dtRaw, 0.05);
    Game.t += dt;
    Game.frame++;
    const p = Game.player;
    const st = stats();

    if (!Game.victoryScored && Game.t >= CFG.VICTORY_SECOND) {
      Game.victoryScored = true;
      Game.victory = true;
      finishRun(true);
      Game.state = 'victory';
      showBanner('ПОБЕДА! Вы выжили 12 минут', '#ffe066');
      emit('victory');
      return;
    }

    /* --- movement --- */
    let ix = Game.input.kx, iy = Game.input.ky;
    if (Game.input.active) {
      const dx = Game.input.x - (p.x - Game.view.camX);
      const dy = Game.input.y - (p.y - Game.view.camY);
      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
      if (d > 8) { ix = dx / d; iy = dy / d; }
    }
    const il = Math.hypot(ix, iy);
    if (il > 1) { ix /= il; iy /= il; }

    const accel = 2600;
    p.vx += ix * accel * dt;
    p.vy += iy * accel * dt;
    const fr = Math.pow(0.0009, dt);
    p.vx *= fr; p.vy *= fr;
    const sp = Math.hypot(p.vx, p.vy);
    const maxS = st.speed;
    if (sp > maxS) { p.vx = (p.vx / sp) * maxS; p.vy = (p.vy / sp) * maxS; }
    p.x += p.vx * dt; p.y += p.vy * dt;
    if (Math.abs(p.vx) > 12) p.facing = p.vx > 0 ? 1 : -1;

    if (sp > 40) {
      p.trail.push({ x: p.x, y: p.y, life: 0.4 });
      if (p.trail.length > 16) p.trail.shift();
    }
    for (const t of p.trail) t.life -= dt;

    p.iFrame -= dt;
    if (st.regen > 0 && p.hp < p.maxHp) p.hp = Math.min(p.maxHp, p.hp + st.regen * dt);

    /* --- camera --- */
    Game.view.camX = lerp(Game.view.camX, p.x - Game.view.w / 2, 1 - Math.pow(0.001, dt));
    Game.view.camY = lerp(Game.view.camY, p.y - Game.view.h / 2, 1 - Math.pow(0.001, dt));

    /* --- spawn --- */
    spawnWave(dt);

    /* --- weapons --- */
    fireWeapons(dt);

    /* --- enemies --- */
    const alive = Game.enemies;
    for (let i = 0; i < alive.length; i++) {
      const e = alive[i];
      if (e.dead) continue;
      e.spawnT += dt;
      e.hitFlash -= dt;
      e.slowT -= dt;
      if (e.slowT <= 0) e.slowAmt = 0;

      const dx = p.x - e.x, dy = p.y - e.y;
      const dist = Math.hypot(dx, dy) || 1;
      let speed = e.speed * (1 - e.slowAmt);

      let mvx = (dx / dist) * speed;
      let mvy = (dy / dist) * speed;

      if (e.def.wobble) {
        e.wobbleA += dt * e.def.wobble;
        const px = -dy / dist, py = dx / dist;
        const w = Math.sin(e.wobbleA) * 0.6;
        mvx += px * speed * w;
        mvy += py * speed * w;
      }

      e.x += mvx * dt + e.knockX * dt;
      e.y += mvy * dt + e.knockY * dt;
      const kd = Math.pow(0.0006, dt);
      e.knockX *= kd; e.knockY *= kd;

      // separation (cheap, sampled) so the horde reads as a crowd
      if ((Game.frame + i) % 3 === 0) {
        for (let j = i + 1; j < Math.min(i + 12, alive.length); j++) {
          const o = alive[j];
          if (o.dead) continue;
          const ox = o.x - e.x, oy = o.y - e.y;
          const od = ox * ox + oy * oy;
          const minD = e.r + o.r;
          if (od < minD * minD && od > 0.01) {
            const d = Math.sqrt(od);
            const push = ((minD - d) / d) * 0.5;
            e.x -= ox * push; e.y -= oy * push;
            o.x += ox * push; o.y += oy * push;
          }
        }
      }

      // ranged
      if (e.def.ranged) {
        e.cd -= dt;
        if (e.cd <= 0 && dist < 620) {
          e.cd = e.def.cd;
          const n = e.def.isBoss ? 6 : e.def.isElite ? 3 : 1;
          const base = Math.atan2(dy, dx);
          for (let k = 0; k < n; k++) {
            const a = base + (n === 1 ? 0 : (k - (n - 1) / 2) * 0.34);
            Game.ebullets.push({
              x: e.x, y: e.y, vx: Math.cos(a) * e.def.projSpeed, vy: Math.sin(a) * e.def.projSpeed,
              r: e.def.isBoss ? 9 : 6, dmg: e.dmg * 0.7, life: 4, color: e.color,
            });
          }
        }
      }

      // boss summon
      if (e.def.summon) {
        e.summonT -= dt;
        if (e.summonT <= 0) {
          e.summonT = 5;
          for (let k = 0; k < 4; k++) {
            const a = Math.random() * TAU;
            spawnEnemy(pick(['drifter', 'darter', 'ghost']), e.x + Math.cos(a) * 70, e.y + Math.sin(a) * 70);
          }
          burst(e.x, e.y, 20, e.color, 220);
        }
      }

      // contact damage
      if (dist < e.r + p.r) {
        hurtPlayer(e.dmg, e);
        const a = Math.atan2(-dy, -dx);
        e.knockX += Math.cos(a) * 60; e.knockY += Math.sin(a) * 60;
      }
    }
    // cull
    if (Game.frame % 20 === 0) {
      Game.enemies = Game.enemies.filter((e) => !e.dead);
    }

    /* --- player bullets --- */
    for (let i = Game.bullets.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const b = Game.bullets[i];
      b.life -= dt;
      if (b.life <= 0) { Game.bullets.splice(i, 1); continue; }

      if (b.homing) {
        const tgt = nearestEnemy(b.x, b.y, 420);
        if (tgt) {
          const a = Math.atan2(tgt.y - b.y, tgt.x - b.x);
          const ca = Math.atan2(b.vy, b.vx);
          let diff = a - ca;
          while (diff > Math.PI) diff -= TAU;
          while (diff < -Math.PI) diff += TAU;
          const na = ca + clamp(diff, -b.homing * dt, b.homing * dt);
          b.vx = Math.cos(na) * b.speed;
          b.vy = Math.sin(na) * b.speed;
        }
      }
      if (b.kind === 'swarm') {
        b.wob += dt * 8;
        const px = -b.vy, py = b.vx;
        const l = Math.hypot(px, py) || 1;
        b.x += (px / l) * Math.sin(b.wob) * 40 * dt;
        b.y += (py / l) * Math.sin(b.wob) * 40 * dt;
      }
      b.x += b.vx * dt; b.y += b.vy * dt;

      let removed = false;
      for (let j = 0; j < Game.enemies.length; j++) {
        const e = Game.enemies[j];
        if (e.dead || b.hit.has(e)) continue;
        const dx = e.x - b.x, dy = e.y - b.y;
        if (dx * dx + dy * dy < (e.r + b.r) * (e.r + b.r)) {
          b.hit.add(e);
          damageEnemy(e, b.dmg, { showDmg: true, knock: b.kind === 'spike' ? 30 : 0 });
          burst(b.x, b.y, 4, b.color, 120);
          global.Audio2 && global.Audio2.SFX.hit();
          if (b.pierce > 0) { b.pierce--; } else { Game.bullets.splice(i, 1); removed = true; }
          break;
        }
      }
      if (removed) continue;
    }

    /* --- enemy bullets --- */
    for (let i = Game.ebullets.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const b = Game.ebullets[i];
      b.life -= dt;
      b.x += b.vx * dt; b.y += b.vy * dt;
      if (b.life <= 0) { Game.ebullets.splice(i, 1); continue; }
      const dx = p.x - b.x, dy = p.y - b.y;
      if (dx * dx + dy * dy < (p.r + b.r) * (p.r + b.r)) {
        hurtPlayer(b.dmg);
        burst(b.x, b.y, 6, b.color, 140);
        Game.ebullets.splice(i, 1);
      }
    }

    /* --- novae --- */
    for (let i = Game.novae.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const n = Game.novae[i];
      n.life -= dt;
      if (n.life <= 0) { Game.novae.splice(i, 1); continue; }
      if (n.bolt) continue;
      const k = 1 - n.life / n.max;
      n.r = 20 + (n.maxR - 20) * (1 - Math.pow(1 - k, 2));
      for (let j = 0; j < Game.enemies.length; j++) {
        const e = Game.enemies[j];
        if (e.dead || n.hit.has(e)) continue;
        const dx = e.x - n.x, dy = e.y - n.y;
        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
        if (d < n.r + e.r) {
          n.hit.add(e);
          damageEnemy(e, n.dmg, { knock: n.knock, angle: Math.atan2(dy, dx), slow: n.slow, slowTime: n.slowTime, showDmg: true });
          burst(e.x, e.y, 5, n.color, 160);
          if (n.chain && n.chain > 0) {
            Game.novae.push({ x: e.x, y: e.y, r: 14, maxR: n.maxR * 0.55, life: 0.4, max: 0.4, dmg: n.dmg * 0.6, knock: n.knock * 0.7, color: n.color, hit: new Set(), chain: n.chain - 1 });
          }
        }
      }
    }

    /* --- traps --- */
    for (let i = Game.traps.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const t = Game.traps[i];
      t.life -= dt;
      t.tickT -= dt;
      t.pulse += dt;
      if (t.life <= 0) { Game.traps.splice(i, 1); continue; }
      if (t.tickT <= 0) {
        t.tickT = t.tick;
        for (let j = 0; j < Game.enemies.length; j++) {
          const e = Game.enemies[j];
          if (e.dead) continue;
          const dx = e.x - t.x, dy = e.y - t.y;
          if (dx * dx + dy * dy < (t.r + e.r) * (t.r + e.r)) {
            damageEnemy(e, t.dmg, { slow: 0.25, slowTime: 0.4 });
          }
        }
      }
    }

    /* --- gems --- */
    for (let i = Game.gems.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const g = Game.gems[i];
      g.life += dt;
      g.x += g.vx * dt; g.y += g.vy * dt;
      g.vx *= 0.9; g.vy *= 0.9;
      const dx = p.x - g.x, dy = p.y - g.y;
      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
      if (d < st.pickup) {
        const pull = 420 + (st.pickup - d) * 6;
        g.x += (dx / d) * pull * dt;
        g.y += (dy / d) * pull * dt;
      }
      if (d < p.r + 6) {
        addXp(g.v);
        global.Audio2 && global.Audio2.SFX.xp();
        Game.gems.splice(i, 1);
      } else if (g.life > 60) {
        Game.gems.splice(i, 1);
      }
    }
    if (Game.gems.length > 700) Game.gems.splice(0, Game.gems.length - 700);

    /* --- chests --- */
    for (let i = Game.chestDrops.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const c = Game.chestDrops[i];
      c.life -= dt; c.pulse += dt;
      if (c.life <= 0) { Game.chestDrops.splice(i, 1); continue; }
      const dx = p.x - c.x, dy = p.y - c.y;
      if (dx * dx + dy * dy < (p.r + c.r) * (p.r + c.r)) {
        Game.chestDrops.splice(i, 1);
        Game.offerQueue++;
        Game.score += 300;
        burst(c.x, c.y, 30, '#ffe066', 300);
        global.Audio2 && global.Audio2.SFX.chest();
        showBanner('СУНДУК! Выберите награду', '#ffe066');
      }
    }

    /* --- particles --- */
    for (let i = Game.particles.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const q = Game.particles[i];
      q.life -= dt;
      if (q.life <= 0) { Game.particles.splice(i, 1); continue; }
      q.x += q.vx * dt; q.y += q.vy * dt;
      q.vx *= 0.92; q.vy *= 0.92;
    }

    /* --- floaters --- */
    for (let i = Game.floaters.length - 1; i >= 0; i--) {
      const f = Game.floaters[i];
      f.life -= dt; f.y -= 34 * dt;
      if (f.life <= 0) Game.floaters.splice(i, 1);
    }

    /* --- screen fx --- */
    if (Game.shake > 0) Game.shake = Math.max(0, Game.shake - dt * 42);
    if (Game.flash > 0) Game.flash = Math.max(0, Game.flash - dt * 2.2);
    if (Game.bannerT > 0) { Game.bannerT -= dt; if (Game.bannerT <= 0) Game.banner = null; }
  };

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * meta helpers exposed to UI
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  Game.loadMeta = async function () {
    let data = null;
    try { data = await global.SDK.loadData(); } catch (e) { data = null; }
    if (!data || data.v !== DEFAULT_META.v) {
      data = Object.assign(freshMeta(), data || {});
      data.upgrades = data.upgrades || {};
      data.unlocked = data.unlocked || { larva: true };
    }
    Game.meta = data;
    return data;
  };

  /** Offline progress: idle games live on this. Capped at 8h. */
  Game.claimOffline = function () {
    const now = Date.now();
    const last = Game.meta.lastSeen || now;
    const elapsed = Math.min((now - last) / 1000, 8 * 3600);
    Game.meta.lastSeen = now;
    if (elapsed < 300) return null; // under 5 min -> nothing to show
    const perMin = 2.2 + Object.keys(Game.meta.upgrades).length * 0.55;
    const amount = Math.floor(elapsed / 60 * perMin);
    if (amount < 5) return null;
    Game.meta.biomass += amount;
    Game.meta.totalBiomass += amount;
    return { minutes: Math.floor(elapsed / 60), amount };
  };

  Game.buyUpgrade = function (id) {
    const item = CFG.META.find((m) => m.id === id);
    if (!item) return false;
    const lvl = Game.meta.upgrades[id] || 0;
    if (lvl >= item.max) return false;
    const cost = CFG.metaCost(item, lvl);
    if (Game.meta.biomass < cost) return false;
    Game.meta.biomass -= cost;
    Game.meta.upgrades[id] = lvl + 1;
    global.SDK.saveData(Game.meta);
    global.Audio2 && global.Audio2.SFX.coin();
    return true;
  };

  Game.isCharUnlocked = function (id) { return !!Game.meta.unlocked[id]; };

  global.Game = Game;
})(typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window : globalThis);
