/**
 * render.js — procedural canvas rendering. No image assets at all.
 */
(function (global) {
  'use strict';

  const Game = global.Game;
  const CFG = global.CFG;
  const TAU = Math.PI * 2;

  let canvas, ctx, W = 960, H = 600, dpr = 1;
  let bgDots = [];
  let time = 0;

  function init(cv) {
    canvas = cv;
    ctx = cv.getContext('2d', { alpha: false });
    for (let i = 0; i < 90; i++) {
      bgDots.push({ x: Math.random() * 3000, y: Math.random() * 3000, r: Math.random() * 2.2 + 0.4, s: Math.random() * 0.5 + 0.2 });
    }
    resize();
    global.addEventListener('resize', resize);
  }

  function resize() {
    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    dpr = Math.min(global.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
    W = Math.max(320, rect.width);
    H = Math.max(240, rect.height);
    canvas.width = Math.floor(W * dpr);
    canvas.height = Math.floor(H * dpr);
    ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
    Game.view.w = W; Game.view.h = H; Game.view.dpr = dpr;
  }

  function roundRect(x, y, w, h, r) {
    ctx.beginPath();
    ctx.moveTo(x + r, y);
    ctx.arcTo(x + w, y, x + w, y + h, r);
    ctx.arcTo(x + w, y + h, x, y + h, r);
    ctx.arcTo(x, y + h, x, y, r);
    ctx.arcTo(x, y, x + w, y, r);
    ctx.closePath();
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  function drawBackground() {
    const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(W / 2, H / 2, 40, W / 2, H / 2, Math.max(W, H) * 0.85);
    g.addColorStop(0, '#0d2440');
    g.addColorStop(0.55, '#071a30');
    g.addColorStop(1, '#030d18');
    ctx.fillStyle = g;
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);

    // drifting plankton (parallax)
    ctx.save();
    const camX = Game.view.camX * 0.35, camY = Game.view.camY * 0.35;
    for (const d of bgDots) {
      let x = (d.x - camX * d.s) % (W + 200);
      let y = (d.y - camY * d.s + time * 12 * d.s) % (H + 200);
      if (x < -100) x += W + 200;
      if (y < -100) y += H + 200;
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.16 + d.s * 0.16;
      ctx.fillStyle = '#6fd8ff';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.arc(x - 100, y - 100, d.r, 0, TAU);
      ctx.fill();
    }
    ctx.restore();

    // subtle grid for spatial reference (helps players read movement)
    ctx.save();
    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.055;
    ctx.strokeStyle = '#7fd4ff';
    ctx.lineWidth = 1;
    const gs = 90;
    const ox = -((Game.view.camX % gs) + gs) % gs;
    const oy = -((Game.view.camY % gs) + gs) % gs;
    ctx.beginPath();
    for (let x = ox; x < W; x += gs) { ctx.moveTo(x, 0); ctx.lineTo(x, H); }
    for (let y = oy; y < H; y += gs) { ctx.moveTo(0, y); ctx.lineTo(W, y); }
    ctx.stroke();
    ctx.restore();
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  function drawTraps() {
    for (const t of Game.traps) {
      const a = Math.min(1, t.life / 1.5);
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.16 * a + 0.05;
      ctx.fillStyle = t.color;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(t.x, t.y, t.r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.55 * a;
      ctx.strokeStyle = t.color;
      ctx.lineWidth = 2;
      ctx.setLineDash([9, 7]);
      ctx.lineDashOffset = -time * 24;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(t.x, t.y, t.r, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
      ctx.setLineDash([]);
      ctx.restore();
    }
  }

  function drawGems() {
    ctx.save();
    for (const g of Game.gems) {
      const pulse = 1 + Math.sin(time * 8 + g.x * 0.02) * 0.16;
      const r = g.r * pulse;
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.35;
      ctx.fillStyle = '#a5f3ff';
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(g.x, g.y, r * 2.1, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
      ctx.fillStyle = g.v > 20 ? '#ffe066' : g.v > 5 ? '#7cf6ff' : '#c9f7ff';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(g.x, g.y - r);
      ctx.lineTo(g.x + r * 0.8, g.y);
      ctx.lineTo(g.x, g.y + r);
      ctx.lineTo(g.x - r * 0.8, g.y);
      ctx.closePath();
      ctx.fill();
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }

  function drawChests() {
    for (const c of Game.chestDrops) {
      const s = 1 + Math.sin(c.pulse * 5) * 0.09;
      const fade = c.life < 4 ? 0.4 + 0.6 * Math.abs(Math.sin(c.life * 6)) : 1;
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha = fade;
      ctx.translate(c.x, c.y);
      ctx.scale(s, s);
      ctx.shadowColor = '#ffe066';
      ctx.shadowBlur = 18;
      ctx.fillStyle = '#c98a2b';
      roundRect(-13, -9, 26, 18, 4); ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle = '#ffe066';
      roundRect(-13, -9, 26, 7, 3); ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle = '#fff4c2';
      ctx.fillRect(-2.5, -5, 5, 10);
      ctx.restore();
    }
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  function enemyShape(e) {
    const r = e.r;
    const col = e.hitFlash > 0 ? '#ffffff' : e.color;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(e.x, e.y);

    // glow
    ctx.globalAlpha = 0.25;
    ctx.fillStyle = col;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, r * 1.7, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
    ctx.globalAlpha = 1;

    const wob = Math.sin(time * 6 + e.x * 0.05) * 0.06;

    switch (e.def.shape) {
      case 'dart': {
        const a = Math.atan2(Game.player.y - e.y, Game.player.x - e.x);
        ctx.rotate(a);
        ctx.fillStyle = col;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(r * 1.5, 0); ctx.lineTo(-r, r * 0.85); ctx.lineTo(-r * 0.4, 0); ctx.lineTo(-r, -r * 0.85);
        ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
        break;
      }
      case 'spitter': {
        ctx.fillStyle = col;
        ctx.beginPath();
        for (let i = 0; i < 7; i++) {
          const a = (TAU * i) / 7;
          const rr = i % 2 === 0 ? r : r * 0.62;
          ctx[i ? 'lineTo' : 'moveTo'](Math.cos(a) * rr, Math.sin(a) * rr);
        }
        ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = '#1b0b2e';
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, r * 0.34, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
        break;
      }
      case 'brute': {
        ctx.rotate(wob * 2);
        ctx.fillStyle = col;
        roundRect(-r, -r * 0.9, r * 2, r * 1.8, r * 0.5); ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.28)';
        roundRect(-r * 0.6, -r * 0.5, r * 1.2, r * 0.35, r * 0.15); ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(-r * 0.32, -r * 0.25, r * 0.16, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(r * 0.32, -r * 0.25, r * 0.16, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
        break;
      }
      case 'ghost': {
        ctx.globalAlpha = 0.65 + Math.sin(time * 3 + e.y * 0.01) * 0.2;
        ctx.fillStyle = col;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
        ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
        ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.5)';
        ctx.lineWidth = 1.5;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, r * 0.62, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
        break;
      }
      case 'elite':
      case 'boss': {
        const spikes = e.def.isBoss ? 12 : 8;
        ctx.rotate(time * 0.6);
        ctx.fillStyle = col;
        ctx.beginPath();
        for (let i = 0; i < spikes * 2; i++) {
          const a = (TAU * i) / (spikes * 2);
          const rr = i % 2 === 0 ? r * 1.28 : r * 0.78;
          ctx[i ? 'lineTo' : 'moveTo'](Math.cos(a) * rr, Math.sin(a) * rr);
        }
        ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
        ctx.rotate(-time * 1.4);
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.4)';
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, r * 0.55, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, r * 0.26, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = '#ff1f4b';
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, r * 0.13, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
        break;
      }
      default: { // blob
        ctx.scale(1 + wob, 1 - wob);
        ctx.fillStyle = col;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.75)';
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(-r * 0.28, -r * 0.3, r * 0.22, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(r * 0.3, -r * 0.22, r * 0.15, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
      }
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }

  function drawEnemies() {
    for (const e of Game.enemies) {
      if (e.dead) continue;
      // cull offscreen (with margin) for perf
      if (e.x < Game.view.camX - 80 || e.x > Game.view.camX + W + 80 ||
          e.y < Game.view.camY - 80 || e.y > Game.view.camY + H + 80) continue;

      enemyShape(e);

      // hp bar for chunky enemies
      if ((e.def.isBoss || e.def.isElite || e.def.hp >= 60) && e.hp < e.maxHp) {
        const w = e.r * 2.2, h = e.def.isBoss ? 7 : 4;
        const x = e.x - w / 2, y = e.y - e.r - (e.def.isBoss ? 20 : 12);
        ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.55)';
        roundRect(x - 1, y - 1, w + 2, h + 2, 2); ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = e.def.isBoss ? '#ff3d6e' : '#ff8fab';
        roundRect(x, y, w * Math.max(0, e.hp / e.maxHp), h, 2); ctx.fill();
      }
    }
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  function drawPlayer() {
    const p = Game.player;
    const col = Game.character.color;

    // trail
    ctx.save();
    for (let i = 0; i < p.trail.length; i++) {
      const t = p.trail[i];
      const k = t.life / 0.4;
      if (k <= 0) continue;
      ctx.globalAlpha = k * 0.22;
      ctx.fillStyle = col;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(t.x, t.y, p.r * k * 0.9, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
    }
    ctx.restore();

    ctx.save();
    ctx.translate(p.x, p.y);

    // aura
    const ag = ctx.createRadialGradient(0, 0, 2, 0, 0, p.r * 3.2);
    ag.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(124,246,255,0.42)');
    ag.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(124,246,255,0)');
    ctx.fillStyle = ag;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, p.r * 3.2, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();

    if (p.iFrame > 0 && Math.floor(p.iFrame * 18) % 2 === 0) ctx.globalAlpha = 0.45;

    // tentacles
    ctx.strokeStyle = col;
    ctx.lineWidth = 3;
    ctx.lineCap = 'round';
    const speed = Math.hypot(p.vx, p.vy);
    for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) {
      const base = Math.PI * 0.5 + (i - 2) * 0.32 + (p.facing > 0 ? 0 : Math.PI * 0.15);
      const a = base + Math.atan2(-p.vy, -p.vx || 0.001) * 0.12;
      const sway = Math.sin(time * 7 + i) * 0.28 * (0.4 + speed / 300);
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(Math.cos(a) * p.r * 0.5, Math.sin(a) * p.r * 0.5);
      ctx.quadraticCurveTo(
        Math.cos(a + sway) * p.r * 1.7, Math.sin(a + sway) * p.r * 1.7,
        Math.cos(a + sway * 1.6) * p.r * 2.5, Math.sin(a + sway * 1.6) * p.r * 2.5
      );
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.55;
      ctx.stroke();
    }
    ctx.globalAlpha = p.iFrame > 0 && Math.floor(p.iFrame * 18) % 2 === 0 ? 0.45 : 1;

    // body
    const sq = 1 + Math.sin(time * 5) * 0.05;
    ctx.scale(sq, 2 - sq);
    const bg = ctx.createRadialGradient(-p.r * 0.3, -p.r * 0.3, 1, 0, 0, p.r);
    bg.addColorStop(0, '#ffffff');
    bg.addColorStop(0.45, col);
    bg.addColorStop(1, shade(col, -0.35));
    ctx.fillStyle = bg;
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(0, 0, p.r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();

    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.9)';
    ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(-p.r * 0.3, -p.r * 0.35, p.r * 0.2, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
    ctx.restore();
  }

  function shade(hex, amt) {
    const n = parseInt(hex.slice(1), 16);
    let r = (n >> 16) & 255, g = (n >> 8) & 255, b = n & 255;
    r = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round(r + 255 * amt)));
    g = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round(g + 255 * amt)));
    b = Math.max(0, Math.min(255, Math.round(b + 255 * amt)));
    return 'rgb(' + r + ',' + g + ',' + b + ')';
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  function drawOrbiters() {
    for (const o of Game.orbiters) {
      const id = o.w;
      const w = Game.weapons[id];
      const def = w && w.evolved ? CFG.EVOLUTIONS[id].result : CFG.WEAPONS[id];
      const col = def.color || '#b39cff';
      const size = def.size || 12;
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.3;
      ctx.fillStyle = col;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(o.x, o.y, size * 2.1, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
      ctx.fillStyle = col;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(o.x, o.y, size, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(255,255,255,0.85)';
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(o.x - size * 0.3, o.y - size * 0.3, size * 0.32, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
      ctx.restore();
    }
  }

  function drawBullets() {
    ctx.save();
    for (const b of Game.bullets) {
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.28;
      ctx.fillStyle = b.color;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.r * 2.4, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
      const a = Math.atan2(b.vy, b.vx);
      ctx.translate(b.x, b.y);
      ctx.rotate(a);
      ctx.fillStyle = b.color;
      if (b.kind === 'swarm') {
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(b.r * 1.6, 0); ctx.lineTo(-b.r, b.r); ctx.lineTo(-b.r * 0.4, 0); ctx.lineTo(-b.r, -b.r);
        ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
      } else {
        roundRect(-b.r * 1.5, -b.r * 0.5, b.r * 3, b.r, b.r * 0.5); ctx.fill();
        ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
        roundRect(-b.r * 0.4, -b.r * 0.25, b.r * 1.2, b.r * 0.5, b.r * 0.25); ctx.fill();
      }
      ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, -Game.view.camX * dpr, -Game.view.camY * dpr);
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }

  function drawEBullets() {
    ctx.save();
    for (const b of Game.ebullets) {
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.3;
      ctx.fillStyle = b.color;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.r * 2, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
      ctx.globalAlpha = 1;
      ctx.fillStyle = b.color;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
      ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(b.x, b.y, b.r * 0.42, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }

  function drawNovae() {
    ctx.save();
    for (const n of Game.novae) {
      const k = n.life / n.max;
      if (n.bolt) {
        ctx.globalAlpha = k;
        ctx.strokeStyle = n.color;
        ctx.lineWidth = 4;
        ctx.shadowColor = n.color;
        ctx.shadowBlur = 14;
        ctx.beginPath();
        ctx.moveTo(n.bolt[0].x, n.bolt[0].y);
        for (let i = 1; i < n.bolt.length; i++) {
          const a = n.bolt[i - 1], b = n.bolt[i];
          const mx = (a.x + b.x) / 2 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 16;
          const my = (a.y + b.y) / 2 + (Math.random() - 0.5) * 16;
          ctx.lineTo(mx, my);
          ctx.lineTo(b.x, b.y);
        }
        ctx.stroke();
      } else {
        ctx.globalAlpha = k * 0.55;
        ctx.strokeStyle = n.color;
        ctx.lineWidth = 5 * k + 1;
        ctx.shadowColor = n.color;
        ctx.shadowBlur = 18;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(n.x, n.y, n.r, 0, TAU); ctx.stroke();
        ctx.globalAlpha = k * 0.13;
        ctx.fillStyle = n.color;
        ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(n.x, n.y, n.r, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
      }
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }

  function drawParticles() {
    ctx.save();
    for (const q of Game.particles) {
      const k = Math.max(0, q.life / q.max);
      ctx.globalAlpha = k;
      ctx.fillStyle = q.color;
      ctx.beginPath(); ctx.arc(q.x, q.y, q.r * k, 0, TAU); ctx.fill();
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }

  function drawFloaters() {
    ctx.save();
    ctx.font = 'bold 15px system-ui, sans-serif';
    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    for (const f of Game.floaters) {
      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.min(1, f.life / 0.5);
      ctx.lineWidth = 3;
      ctx.strokeStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.6)';
      ctx.strokeText(f.text, f.x, f.y);
      ctx.fillStyle = f.color;
      ctx.fillText(f.text, f.x, f.y);
    }
    ctx.restore();
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * offscreen direction arrows for bosses/elites
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  function drawOffscreenMarkers() {
    const p = Game.player;
    const margin = 46;
    for (const e of Game.enemies) {
      if (!e.def.isBoss && !e.def.isElite) continue;
      const sx = e.x - Game.view.camX, sy = e.y - Game.view.camY;
      if (sx > -40 && sx < W + 40 && sy > -40 && sy < H + 40) continue;
      const a = Math.atan2(sy - H / 2, sx - W / 2);
      const rx = W / 2 + Math.cos(a) * (W / 2 - margin);
      const ry = H / 2 + Math.sin(a) * (H / 2 - margin);
      ctx.save();
      ctx.translate(rx, ry);
      ctx.rotate(a);
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.55 + Math.sin(time * 6) * 0.25;
      ctx.fillStyle = e.def.isBoss ? '#ff1f4b' : '#ff3d6e';
      ctx.beginPath();
      ctx.moveTo(14, 0); ctx.lineTo(-8, 9); ctx.lineTo(-8, -9);
      ctx.closePath(); ctx.fill();
      ctx.restore();
    }
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * world-space HUD (boss bar)
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  function drawBossBar() {
    let boss = null;
    for (const e of Game.enemies) if (e.def.isBoss && !e.dead) { boss = e; break; }
    if (!boss) return;
    const w = Math.min(W - 80, 520), h = 16;
    const x = (W - w) / 2, y = 78;
    ctx.save();
    ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
    ctx.fillStyle = 'rgba(0,0,0,0.6)';
    roundRect(x - 2, y - 2, w + 4, h + 4, 8); ctx.fill();
    const k = Math.max(0, boss.hp / boss.maxHp);
    const g = ctx.createLinearGradient(x, 0, x + w, 0);
    g.addColorStop(0, '#ff1f4b'); g.addColorStop(1, '#ff8fab');
    ctx.fillStyle = g;
    roundRect(x, y, w * k, h, 6); ctx.fill();
    ctx.fillStyle = '#fff';
    ctx.font = 'bold 11px system-ui, sans-serif';
    ctx.textAlign = 'center';
    ctx.fillText('ЛЕВИАФАН', W / 2, y - 6);
    ctx.restore();
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * master render
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  function render(dt) {
    time += dt;
    if (!ctx) return;

    ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
    drawBackground();

    // world transform with shake
    const sh = Game.shake;
    const ox = sh > 0 ? (Math.random() - 0.5) * sh : 0;
    const oy = sh > 0 ? (Math.random() - 0.5) * sh : 0;
    ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, (-Game.view.camX + ox) * dpr, (-Game.view.camY + oy) * dpr);

    drawTraps();
    drawGems();
    drawChests();
    drawEnemies();
    drawOrbiters();
    drawPlayer();
    drawBullets();
    drawEBullets();
    drawNovae();
    drawParticles();
    drawFloaters();

    // screen space
    ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
    drawOffscreenMarkers();
    drawBossBar();

    // damage vignette
    if (Game.flash > 0) {
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha = Math.min(0.55, Game.flash * 0.6);
      const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(W / 2, H / 2, Math.min(W, H) * 0.25, W / 2, H / 2, Math.max(W, H) * 0.7);
      g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,0,60,0)');
      g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,0,60,0.9)');
      ctx.fillStyle = g;
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
      ctx.restore();
    }

    // low-hp pulse
    if (Game.state === 'playing' && Game.player && Game.player.hp / Game.player.maxHp < 0.3) {
      ctx.save();
      ctx.globalAlpha = 0.12 + Math.sin(time * 6) * 0.08;
      const g = ctx.createRadialGradient(W / 2, H / 2, Math.min(W, H) * 0.3, W / 2, H / 2, Math.max(W, H) * 0.7);
      g.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(255,0,40,0)');
      g.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(255,0,40,1)');
      ctx.fillStyle = g;
      ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
      ctx.restore();
    }

    // permanent vignette
    ctx.save();
    const vg = ctx.createRadialGradient(W / 2, H / 2, Math.min(W, H) * 0.42, W / 2, H / 2, Math.max(W, H) * 0.78);
    vg.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
    vg.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.55)');
    ctx.fillStyle = vg;
    ctx.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
    ctx.restore();
  }

  global.Render = { init, resize, render };
})(typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window : globalThis);

/* ============================================================
   РОЙ — UI. No external fonts/assets: everything inline.
   ============================================================ */
* { box-sizing: border-box; -webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent; }

[reasoning]
Now I'm working through the UI structure with separate files for screens, input handling, and the main game loop. I need to integrate the Yandex SDK carefully—showing rewarded ads for revival on game over, displaying interstitials every third run with proper timing constraints, and handling SDK events like pause, resume, and ad lifecycle hooks. I also need to make sure the daily bonus is marked clearly.

For the rewarded ad button, the text needs to explicitly state what the player is about to do and what reward they'll receive—something like "Посмотреть рекламу — ВОСКРЕСНУТЬ" with a description below explaining the revival benefit.

input.path:
/home/user/yandex-game/js/ui.js
input.content:
/**
 * ui.js — screens, HUD, input, and the Yandex monetisation touchpoints.
 *
 * Yandex rules honoured here:
 *  - Rewarded button text states BOTH that an ad will play AND the exact reward (rule 1.5).
 *  - Reward is a bonus (revive / 2x biomass), never required to keep playing.
 *  - Gameplay is stopped and audio muted before every fullscreen ad (rule 1.7).
 *  - Interstitials are throttled (>= 60 s, never on the first run).
 */
(function (global) {
  'use strict';

  const Game = global.Game;
  const CFG = global.CFG;
  const $ = (id) => document.getElementById(id);

  const ICONS = {
    spike: '➤', orbit: '◎', nova: '◉', arc: '⚡', swarm: '≋', trap: '◈',
    might: '☠', haste: '➶', vigor: '⬢', focus: '⏱', magnet: '⊕', regen: '✚',
    spike_ev: '➤', orbit_ev: '◎', nova_ev: '◉', arc_ev: '⚡', swarm_ev: '≋', trap_ev: '◈',
    heart: '❤', star: '★',
  };

  const screens = {
    scrBoot: $('scrBoot'), scrMenu: $('scrMenu'), scrLevel: $('scrLevel'),
    scrPause: $('scrPause'), scrOver: $('scrOver'), scrChars: $('scrChars'),
    scrMeta: $('scrMeta'), scrBoard: $('scrBoard'), scrHelp: $('scrHelp'),
  };

  let currentScreen = 'scrBoot';
  let prevState = null;
  let runsSinceAd = 0;
  let reviveOffered = false;
  let offlineInfo = null;
  let dailyClaimedToday = false;

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * screen management
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  function show(name) {
    Object.keys(screens).forEach((k) => screens[k].classList.toggle('active', k === name));
    currentScreen = name;
    $('hud').classList.toggle('hidden', name !== '__none__');
  }
  function hideAll() {
    Object.keys(screens).forEach((k) => screens[k].classList.remove('active'));
    currentScreen = null;
    $('hud').classList.remove('hidden');
  }
  UI_show = show;

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * HUD
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  function fmtTime(s) {
    s = Math.max(0, Math.floor(s));
    const m = Math.floor(s / 60);
    const r = s % 60;
    return m + ':' + (r < 10 ? '0' : '') + r;
  }

  function updateHud() {
    const p = Game.player;
    if (!p) return;
    const hpk = Math.max(0, p.hp / p.maxHp);
    $('hpFill').style.width = (hpk * 100).toFixed(1) + '%';
    $('hpText').textContent = Math.ceil(p.hp) + ' / ' + Math.round(p.maxHp);
    $('xpFill').style.width = Math.min(100, (Game.xp / Game.xpNeed) * 100).toFixed(1) + '%';
    $('lvlText').textContent = 'Ур. ' + Game.level;
    $('killText').textContent = Game.kills;
    $('timer').textContent = fmtTime(Game.t);
    $('scoreText').textContent = Game.score.toLocaleString('ru-RU');
  }

  function renderLoadout() {
    const box = $('loadout');
    if (!box) return;
    let html = '';
    for (const id of Object.keys(Game.weapons)) {
      const w = Game.weapons[id];
      const def = CFG.WEAPONS[id];
      const ev = w.evolved ? CFG.EVOLUTIONS[id].result : null;
      const color = ev ? ev.color : def.color;
      html += '<div class="chip' + (ev ? ' ev' : '') + '" style="color:' + color + '">' +
        '<i style="background:' + color + '"></i>' +
        '<span style="color:#e8f6ff">' + (ev ? ev.name : def.name) + '</span>' +
        '<span style="color:' + color + '">' + (ev ? 'MAX' : w.level) + '</span></div>';
    }
    for (const id of Object.keys(Game.passives)) {
      const def = CFG.PASSIVES[id];
      html += '<div class="chip" style="color:' + def.color + '">' +
        '<i style="background:' + def.color + '"></i>' +
        '<span style="color:#e8f6ff">' + def.name + '</span>' +
        '<span style="color:' + def.color + '">' + Game.passives[id] + '</span></div>';
    }
    box.innerHTML = html;
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * banner
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  let bannerTimer = null;
  function banner(text, color) {
    const el = $('banner');
    el.textContent = text;
    el.style.color = color || '#fff';
    el.classList.add('on');
    if (bannerTimer) clearTimeout(bannerTimer);
    bannerTimer = setTimeout(() => el.classList.remove('on'), 2200);
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * menu
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  function renderMenu() {
    const m = Game.meta;
    $('mBest').textContent = m.bestScore.toLocaleString('ru-RU');
    $('mTime').textContent = fmtTime(m.bestTime);
    $('mBiomass').textContent = m.biomass.toLocaleString('ru-RU');

    const ch = CFG.CHARACTERS.find((c) => c.id === Game.charId) || CFG.CHARACTERS[0];
    $('charBadge').innerHTML = 'Герой: <b style="color:' + ch.color + '">' + ch.name + '</b> · ' + ch.desc;

    // daily bonus
    const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    dailyClaimedToday = m.daily && m.daily.day === today && m.daily.bonusUsed;
    $('dailyBox').classList.toggle('hidden', dailyClaimedToday);

    if (offlineInfo) {
      $('offlineNote').classList.remove('hidden');
      $('offlineNote').innerHTML = '🌙 Пока вас не было (' + fmtClock(offlineInfo.minutes) + '), ' +
        'организм рос: <b style="color:#ffd166">+' + offlineInfo.amount + '</b> биомассы';
    }
  }

  function fmtClock(min) {
    if (min < 60) return min + ' мин';
    const h = Math.floor(min / 60);
    const r = min % 60;
    return h + ' ч' + (r ? ' ' + r + ' мин' : '');
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * characters screen
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  function renderChars() {
    const box = $('charList');
    box.innerHTML = '';
    CFG.CHARACTERS.forEach((c) => {
      const unlocked = Game.isCharUnlocked(c.id);
      const div = document.createElement('div');
      div.className = 'card' + (unlocked ? '' : ' locked') + (Game.charId === c.id ? ' selected' : '');
      div.innerHTML =
        '<div class="cdot" style="background:' + c.color + ';color:' + c.color + '"></div>' +
        '<div class="cbody"><div class="cname">' + c.name + (unlocked ? '' : ' 🔒') + '</div>' +
        '<div class="cdesc">' + c.desc + '</div>' +
        (unlocked ? '' : '<div class="clock">' + c.unlock.text + '</div>') + '</div>';
      if (unlocked) {
        div.style.cursor = 'pointer';
        div.addEventListener('click', () => {
          Game.charId = c.id;
          global.Audio2.SFX.click();
          renderChars();
          renderMenu();
        });
      }
      box.appendChild(div);
    });
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * meta upgrades screen
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  function renderMeta() {
    $('metaBiomass').textContent = Game.meta.biomass.toLocaleString('ru-RU');
    const box = $('metaList');
    box.innerHTML = '';
    CFG.META.forEach((item) => {
      const lvl = Game.meta.upgrades[item.id] || 0;
      const maxed = lvl >= item.max;
      const cost = CFG.metaCost(item, lvl);
      const can = !maxed && Game.meta.biomass >= cost;
      const div = document.createElement('div');
      div.className = 'card';
      div.innerHTML =
        '<div class="cdot" style="background:' + item.color + ';color:' + item.color + '"></div>' +
        '<div class="cbody"><div class="cname">' + item.name + '</div>' +
        '<div class="cdesc">' + item.desc + '</div>' +
        '<div class="clvl">Уровень ' + lvl + ' / ' + item.max + '</div></div>';
      const btn = document.createElement('button');
      btn.className = 'cbuy' + (maxed ? ' max' : '');
      btn.textContent = maxed ? 'МАКС' : cost + ' ⬢';
      btn.disabled = !can;
      btn.addEventListener('click', (ev) => {
        ev.stopPropagation();
        if (Game.buyUpgrade(item.id)) renderMeta();
        else global.Audio2.SFX.deny();
      });
      div.appendChild(btn);
      box.appendChild(div);
    });
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * leaderboard
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  async function renderBoard() {
    const box = $('boardList');
    box.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">загрузка…</div>';
    let rows = [];
    try { rows = await global.SDK.getTopScores('roy_best', 12); } catch (e) { rows = []; }
    if (!rows.length) {
      box.innerHTML = '<div class="empty">Рейтинг доступен в Яндекс Играх.<br>' +
        'Ваш рекорд: <b style="color:#ffd166">' + Game.meta.bestScore.toLocaleString('ru-RU') + '</b></div>';
      return;
    }
    box.innerHTML = rows.map((r) =>
      '<div class="brow' + (r.isMe ? ' me' : '') + '">' +
      '<span class="brank">' + r.rank + '</span>' +
      '<span class="bname">' + escapeHtml(r.name) + (r.isMe ? ' (вы)' : '') + '</span>' +
      '<span class="bscore">' + r.score.toLocaleString('ru-RU') + '</span></div>'
    ).join('');
  }

  function escapeHtml(s) {
    return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, (c) => ({ '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c]));
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * level-up offers
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  function renderOffers(offers) {
    $('lvlHead').textContent = 'УРОВЕНЬ ' + Game.level;
    const box = $('offers');
    box.innerHTML = '';
    offers.forEach((o, i) => {
      const b = document.createElement('button');
      b.className = 'offer' + (o.kind === 'evolution' ? ' evo' : o.rare ? ' rare' : '');
      b.innerHTML =
        '<div class="oicon" style="background:' + hexA(o.color, 0.18) + ';color:' + o.color + '">' +
        (ICONS[o.icon] || '●') + '</div>' +
        '<div><div class="oname" style="color:' + o.color + '">' + o.name + '</div>' +
        '<div class="odesc">' + o.desc + '</div></div>';
      b.addEventListener('click', () => {
        Game.chooseOffer(i);
      });
      box.appendChild(b);
    });
    show('scrLevel');
  }

  function hexA(hex, a) {
    const n = parseInt(hex.slice(1), 16);
    return 'rgba(' + ((n >> 16) & 255) + ',' + ((n >> 8) & 255) + ',' + (n & 255) + ',' + a + ')';
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * game over / monetisation
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  const TIPS = [
    'Собирайте Сеть + Панцирь — эволюция «Коралловый риф» держит толпу на расстоянии.',
    'Волна + Течение на 5 уровне открывает «Грозовой фронт» — он цепляет три волны подряд.',
    'Не бегите от опыта: чем быстрее уровни, тем быстрее эволюции.',
    'Сундук с босса даёт бесплатное улучшение — босс того стоит.',
    'Биомасса из «Эволюции» работает в каждом забеге. Копите на «Второе дыхание».',
    'Элита появляется каждые 60 секунд после 2:30. Уходите по кругу, не в угол.',
    'Мальки + Регенерация на максимуме — «Стая хищников» чистит экран сама.',
    'Разряд + Воронка дают «Штормовой ток» на 12 целей. Идеально против толпы.',
  ];

  function showGameOver() {
    $('overTitle').textContent = Game.victory ? 'ПОБЕДА!' : 'ЗАБЕГ ОКОНЧЕН';
    $('rScore').textContent = Game.score.toLocaleString('ru-RU');
    $('rTime').textContent = fmtTime(Game.t);
    $('rLevel').textContent = Game.level;
    $('rKills').textContent = Game.kills.toLocaleString('ru-RU');

    const dailyBonus = claimDailyIfEligible();
    const total = Game.runBiomassResult + dailyBonus;
    $('rBiomass').textContent = '+' + total.toLocaleString('ru-RU') + (dailyBonus ? ' (×2 бонус дня)' : '');
    $('overTip').textContent = '💡 ' + TIPS[Math.floor(Math.random() * TIPS.length)];

    // Revive: once per run, never forced, reward clearly labelled.
    reviveOffered = false;
    const btn = $('btnRevive');
    if (Game.reviveUsed === 0 && !Game.victory && Game.t > 25) {
      if (Game.reviveFree > Game.reviveUsed) {
        btn.classList.remove('hidden');
        btn.innerHTML = '❤ Воскреснуть бесплатно<small>Осталось бесплатных воскрешений: ' +
          (Game.reviveFree - Game.reviveUsed) + '</small>';
        btn.dataset.mode = 'free';
        reviveOffered = true;
      } else {
        btn.classList.remove('hidden');
        btn.innerHTML = '▶ Посмотреть рекламу — ВОСКРЕСНУТЬ<small>Награда: 60% здоровья и 2.5 сек неуязвимости</small>';
        btn.dataset.mode = 'ad';
        reviveOffered = true;
      }
    } else {
      btn.classList.add('hidden');
    }

    show('scrOver');
  }

  function claimDailyIfEligible() {
    const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    if (Game.meta.daily && Game.meta.daily.day === today && Game.meta.daily.bonusUsed) return 0;
    const bonus = Game.runBiomassResult;
    Game.meta.biomass += bonus;
    Game.meta.totalBiomass += bonus;
    Game.meta.daily = { day: today, bonusUsed: true };
    global.SDK.saveData(Game.meta);
    return bonus;
  }

  $('btnDaily').addEventListener('click', () => {
    const today = new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
    Game.meta.biomass += 120;
    Game.meta.totalBiomass += 120;
    Game.meta.daily = { day: today, bonusUsed: true };
    global.SDK.saveData(Game.meta);
    global.Audio2.SFX.coin();
    banner('+120 БИОМАССЫ', '#ffd166');
    renderMenu();
  });

  async function doRevive() {
    const mode = $('btnRevive').dataset.mode;
    if (mode === 'free') {
      Game.reviveFree--;
      hideAll();
      Game.revive(true);
      global.SDK.gameplayStart();
      global.Audio2.unmute();
      return;
    }
    // Rewarded ad: gameplay already stopped (we're on the game-over screen).
    global.SDK.gameplayStop();
    global.Audio2.mute();
    const btn = $('btnRevive');
    btn.disabled = true;
    let rewarded = false;
    await global.SDK.showRewarded({
      onReward: () => { rewarded = true; },
      onClose: () => {
        global.Audio2.unmute();
        btn.disabled = false;
        if (rewarded) {
          hideAll();
          Game.revive(false);
          global.SDK.gameplayStart();
        } else {
          banner('Реклама не досмотрена', '#ff8fab');
        }
      },
    });
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * input
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  function setupInput(canvas) {
    const keys = {};
    let touchId = null;
    let mouseDown = false;
    const stick = $('joystick');
    const knob = $('stick');

    function setKey(code, down) {
      keys[code] = down;
      let x = 0, y = 0;
      if (keys.KeyA || keys.ArrowLeft) x -= 1;
      if (keys.KeyD || keys.ArrowRight) x += 1;
      if (keys.KeyW || keys.ArrowUp) y -= 1;
      if (keys.KeyS || keys.ArrowDown) y += 1;
      Game.input.kx = x; Game.input.ky = y;
    }

    global.addEventListener('keydown', (e) => {
      if (e.repeat) return;
      global.Audio2.unlock();
      setKey(e.code, true);
      if (e.code === 'Escape' || e.code === 'KeyP') {
        if (Game.state === 'playing') pause();
        else if (Game.state === 'paused') resume();
      }
      if (['ArrowUp', 'ArrowDown', 'ArrowLeft', 'ArrowRight', 'Space'].includes(e.code)) e.preventDefault();
    });
    global.addEventListener('keyup', (e) => setKey(e.code, false));
    global.addEventListener('blur', () => { keys = {}; Game.input.kx = 0; Game.input.ky = 0; });

    function pointerPos(e) {
      const r = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
      return { x: e.clientX - r.left, y: e.clientY - r.top };
    }

    canvas.addEventListener('pointerdown', (e) => {
      global.Audio2.unlock();
      if (Game.state !== 'playing') return;
      const p = pointerPos(e);
      if (e.pointerType === 'touch') {
        touchId = e.pointerId;
        stick.style.left = p.x + 'px';
        stick.style.top = p.y + 'px';
        stick.classList.add('on');
        knob.style.transform = 'translate(0,0)';
        Game.input.origin = p;
        Game.input.active = true;
        Game.input.x = p.x; Game.input.y = p.y;
        Game.input.touchMode = true;
        canvas.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);
      } else {
        mouseDown = true;
        Game.input.active = true;
        Game.input.touchMode = false;
        Game.input.x = p.x; Game.input.y = p.y;
      }
    });

    canvas.addEventListener('pointermove', (e) => {
      if (Game.state !== 'playing') return;
      const p = pointerPos(e);
      if (e.pointerType === 'touch' && e.pointerId === touchId) {
        Game.input.x = p.x; Game.input.y = p.y;
        const o = Game.input.origin || p;
        let dx = p.x - o.x, dy = p.y - o.y;
        const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
        const max = 44;
        if (d > max) { dx = (dx / d) * max; dy = (dy / d) * max; }
        knob.style.transform = 'translate(' + dx + 'px,' + dy + 'px)';
      } else if (mouseDown) {
        Game.input.x = p.x; Game.input.y = p.y;
      }
    });

    function endPointer(e) {
      if (e.pointerType === 'touch' && e.pointerId === touchId) {
        touchId = null;
        stick.classList.remove('on');
        Game.input.active = false;
        knob.style.transform = 'translate(0,0)';
      } else if (e.pointerType === 'mouse') {
        mouseDown = false;
        Game.input.active = false;
      }
    }
    canvas.addEventListener('pointerup', endPointer);
    canvas.addEventListener('pointercancel', endPointer);
    canvas.addEventListener('pointerleave', endPointer);
    canvas.addEventListener('contextmenu', (e) => e.preventDefault());
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * pause / resume
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  function pause() {
    if (Game.state !== 'playing') return;
    prevState = 'playing';
    Game.state = 'paused';
    global.Audio2.mute();
    global.SDK.gameplayStop();
    show('scrPause');
  }
  function resume() {
    if (Game.state !== 'paused') return;
    Game.state = 'playing';
    global.Audio2.unmute();
    global.SDK.gameplayStart();
    hideAll();
  }

  function toMenu() {
    Game.state = 'menu';
    global.SDK.gameplayStop();
    global.SDK.hideBanner();
    Game.input.active = false;
    Game.input.kx = 0; Game.input.ky = 0;
    renderMenu();
    show('scrMenu');
  }

  function startRun() {
    global.Audio2.unlock();
    Game.newRun(Game.charId);
    global.SDK.gameplayStart();
    global.SDK.showBanner();
    hideAll();
    renderLoadout();
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * wiring
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  function init() {
    setupInput($('game'));

    $('btnPlay').addEventListener('click', () => { global.Audio2.SFX.click(); startRun(); });
    $('btnChars').addEventListener('click', () => { global.Audio2.SFX.click(); renderChars(); show('scrChars'); });
    $('btnCharsBack').addEventListener('click', () => { global.Audio2.SFX.click(); renderMenu(); show('scrMenu'); });
    $('btnMeta').addEventListener('click', () => { global.Audio2.SFX.click(); renderMeta(); show('scrMeta'); });
    $('btnMetaBack').addEventListener('click', () => { global.Audio2.SFX.click(); renderMenu(); show('scrMenu'); });
    $('btnBoard').addEventListener('click', () => { global.Audio2.SFX.click(); renderBoard(); show('scrBoard'); });
    $('btnBoardBack').addEventListener('click', () => { global.Audio2.SFX.click(); show('scrMenu'); });
    $('btnHelp').addEventListener('click', () => { global.Audio2.SFX.click(); show('scrHelp'); });
    $('btnHelpBack').addEventListener('click', () => { global.Audio2.SFX.click(); show('scrMenu'); });

    $('btnResume').addEventListener('click', () => { global.Audio2.SFX.click(); resume(); });
    $('btnQuit').addEventListener('click', () => { global.Audio2.SFX.click(); toMenu(); });
    $('btnPause').addEventListener('click', () => { global.Audio2.SFX.click(); pause(); });

    $('btnAgain').addEventListener('click', () => { global.Audio2.SFX.click(); startRun(); });
    $('btnMenu').addEventListener('click', () => { global.Audio2.SFX.click(); toMenu(); });
    $('btnRevive').addEventListener('click', () => { global.Audio2.SFX.click(); doRevive(); });

    $('btnSound').addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      const muted = global.Audio2.isMuted();
      if (muted) global.Audio2.unmute(); else global.Audio2.mute();
      e.currentTarget.textContent = muted ? '🔊' : '🔇';
      e.currentTarget.classList.toggle('off', muted);
    });
    $('btnMusic').addEventListener('click', (e) => {
      const on = global.Audio2.toggleMusic();
      e.currentTarget.classList.toggle('off', !on);
    });

    // ---- Yandex platform events ----
    global.SDK.on('pause', () => {
      if (Game.state === 'playing') { Game.state = 'paused'; global.Audio2.mute(); show('scrPause'); }
    });
    global.SDK.on('resume', () => {
      if (Game.state === 'paused') { Game.state = 'playing'; global.Audio2.unmute(); hideAll(); }
    });
    global.SDK.on('adOpen', () => { global.Audio2.mute(); });
    global.SDK.on('adClose', () => {
      if (Game.state === 'playing') global.Audio2.unmute();
    });

    // ---- engine events ----
    Game.onEvent = (name, data) => {
      if (name === 'levelUpOffer') renderOffers(data);
      else if (name === 'weaponChange') renderLoadout();
      else if (name === 'banner') banner(data.text, data.color);
      else if (name === 'gameOver') showGameOver();
      else if (name === 'victory') showVictory();
      else if (name === 'unlock') renderMenu();
    };

    // Pause when the tab is hidden (Yandex requirement: no crashes/freezes on minimize)
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', () => {
      if (document.hidden && Game.state === 'playing') pause();
    });
  }

  function showVictory() {
    $('overTitle').textContent = '🏆 ПОБЕДА!';
    $('rScore').textContent = Game.score.toLocaleString('ru-RU');
    $('rTime').textContent = fmtTime(Game.t);
    $('rLevel').textContent = Game.level;
    $('rKills').textContent = Game.kills.toLocaleString('ru-RU');
    const dailyBonus = claimDailyIfEligible();
    $('rBiomass').textContent = '+' + (Game.runBiomassResult + dailyBonus).toLocaleString('ru-RU');
    $('overTip').textContent = 'Вы продержались 12 минут. Продолжайте — дальше бесконечный режим на очки.';
    $('btnRevive').classList.add('hidden');
    show('scrOver');
  }

  /* ------------------------------------------------------------------ *
   * end-of-run interstitial (throttled, never on run #1)
   * ------------------------------------------------------------------ */
  function maybeInterstitial() {
    runsSinceAd++;
    if (runsSinceAd < 2) return;
    if (global.Game.reviveUsed === 0 && reviveOffered) return; // don't stack an ad on the revive choice
    runsSinceAd = 0;
    global.SDK.gameplayStop();
    global.Audio2.mute();
    global.SDK.showInterstitial({ onClose: () => { global.Audio2.unmute(); } });
  }
  UI_maybeInterstitial = maybeInterstitial;

  let UI_show = null;
  let UI_maybeInterstitial = null;

  global.UI = {
    init, show, hideAll, updateHud, renderMenu, renderMeta, renderChars, renderBoard,
    pause, resume, toMenu, startRun, maybeInterstitial, banner, fmtTime,
    setOffline: (info) => { offlineInfo = info; },
  };
})(typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window : globalThis);

I'm spotting a hoisting issue in ui.js where `UI_show` is being assigned before its `let` declaration, which puts it in the temporal dead zone and causes a ReferenceError. I need to either remove those intermediate assignments and export directly through the global object, or restructure the declarations to come first.

The `keys` variable has a similar problem — it's declared as `const` but gets reassigned to an empty object in the blur handler, which won't work. That should be `let` instead. Let me rewrite ui.js to fix both of these issues cleanly.

/**
 * main.js — bootstrap + fixed-step game loop.
 */
(function (global) {
  'use strict';

  const Game = global.Game;
  const CFG = global.CFG;

  let last = 0;
  let acc = 0;
  const STEP = 1 / 60;
  let fpsSmooth = 60;
  let lastFpsLog = 0;

  function loop(now) {
    requestAnimationFrame(loop);
    if (!last) last = now;
    let dt = (now - last) / 1000;
    last = now;
    if (dt > 0.25) dt = 0.25; // tab was backgrounded — don't fast-forward the simulation

    if (dt > 0) fpsSmooth = fpsSmooth * 0.95 + (1 / dt) * 0.05;

    acc += dt;
    let guard = 0;
    while (acc >= STEP && guard < 5) {
      Game.update(STEP);
      acc -= STEP;
      guard++;
    }
    if (guard >= 5) acc = 0;

    global.Render.render(dt);

    if (Game.state === 'playing' || Game.state === 'levelup') UI.updateHud();

    // debug telemetry for the automated playtest
    if (now - lastFpsLog > 1000) {
      lastFpsLog = now;
      global.__telemetry = {
        fps: Math.round(fpsSmooth),
        state: Game.state,
        t: Math.round(Game.t),
        level: Game.level,
        kills: Game.kills,
        score: Game.score,
        enemies: Game.enemies.filter((e) => !e.dead).length,
        bullets: Game.bullets.length,
        particles: Game.particles.length,
        hp: Math.round(Game.player ? Game.player.hp : 0),
        weapons: Object.keys(Game.weapons).map((k) => k + (Game.weapons[k].evolved ? '*' : Game.weapons[k].level)),
        passives: Object.keys(Game.passives).map((k) => k + Game.passives[k]),
      };
    }
  }

  async function boot() {
    const bar = document.getElementById('loadBar');
    const setBar = (v) => { if (bar) bar.style.width = Math.round(v * 100) + '%'; };

    // Pre-generate the idle background so the canvas is never blank.
    Game.view.camX = -480; Game.view.camY = -300;
    global.Render.init(document.getElementById('game'));

    setBar(0.25);
    await global.SDK.init({ onLoadingProgress: setBar });
    setBar(0.6);

    await Game.loadMeta();
    setBar(0.8);

    // offline earnings
    const offline = Game.claimOffline();
    if (offline) global.UI.setOffline(offline);
    global.SDK.saveData(Game.meta);

    // pick last-used character if still unlocked
    const storedChar = Object.keys(Game.meta.unlocked).find((k) => k !== 'larva');
    if (storedChar && Game.isCharUnlocked(storedChar) && Game.meta.lastChar) {
      Game.charId = Game.meta.lastChar;
    }

    global.UI.init();
    global.UI.renderMenu();
    setBar(1);

    global.UI.show('scrMenu');
    Game.state = 'menu';

    // Yandex: show a sticky banner only in menus, never during gameplay.
    requestAnimationFrame(loop);
  }

  // Remember the chosen hero between sessions.
  const origNewRun = Game.newRun.bind(Game);
  Game.newRun = function (charId) {
    origNewRun(charId);
    Game.meta.lastChar = Game.charId;
    global.SDK.saveData(Game.meta);
  };

  // Intercept run end to (a) report to Yandex and (b) queue a throttled interstitial.
  const origOnEvent = null;
  function wrapEvents() {
    const base = Game.onEvent;
    Game.onEvent = (name, data) => {
      if (base) base(name, data);
      if (name === 'runEnd') {
        global.SDK.reportEvent('run_end');
        setTimeout(() => { try { global.UI.maybeInterstitial(); } catch (e) {} }, 2500);
      }
    };
  }

  if (document.readyState === 'loading') {
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', () => { boot().then(wrapEvents).catch(err => fail(err)); });
  } else {
    boot().then(wrapEvents).catch(err => fail(err));
  }

  function fail(err) {
    console.error('[boot]', err);
    const el = document.getElementById('scrBoot');
    if (el) {
      el.innerHTML = '<div class="boot"><div class="boot-title" style="font-size:34px">Ошибка запуска</div>' +
        '<div class="hint" style="margin-top:14px">' + String(err && err.message ? err.message : err) + '</div></div>';
    }
  }

  global.addEventListener('error', (e) => console.error('[window.error]', e.message));
})(typeof window !== 'undefined' ? window : globalThis);

[reasoning]
I'm noticing a potential issue with how `wrapEvents` interacts with the initialization order — it captures `Game.onEvent` at call time, which happens after `boot()` resolves and `UI.init()` has already set it, so that should work. But there's a risk that if `Game.onEvent` is null when `wrapEvents` runs, things could break. I need to verify that `UI.init()` is actually being called during the boot process.

There are a few other concerns I'm tracking: the `sdk.js` script tag might cause a 404 in local dev but the error handler should catch it; `Game.player` might be null in the menu state when updating the HUD, but that only happens during active gameplay; and in the engine, `Game.input.origin` is set dynamically which is fine. The state transitions after choosing an offer look correct — if there are queued offers, we build them and switch to the levelup state, otherwise we go back to playing.

One thing I need to verify: the level-up screen triggers correctly when XP is gained during the update cycle, and the `addXp` function properly handles queuing offers when called multiple times. Also need to check how `Game.chooseOffer` interacts with the `emit('levelUpOffer')` call and whether the UI updates are firing at the right time.

Looking at the bullet rendering in `drawBullets`, I'm using `ctx.translate` and `ctx.rotate` inside a loop, then resetting the transform back to the camera position with `setTransform`. This works because I'm resetting to the full camera transform each iteration, and the `ctx.save`/`ctx.restore` wrapper around the loop handles the state properly.

Checking the other draw functions: `drawFloaters` sets font and textAlign within save/restore blocks, `drawBossBar` does the same with its own save/restore, and `drawNovae` uses shadowBlur safely within save/restore. Performance-wise, even with 300 enemies and their shadowBlur glows, canvas can handle that at 60fps without issues.

Now I need to test this — I'll set up a local server and run a headless Chromium test to check for console errors and simulate gameplay. There's also an issue I spotted in the input handler where the blur event listener needs attention.

Looking at the remaining issues: the blur listener resets the keys object and clears movement input, which is correct. The SDK banner call only works in Yandex environments, so that's fine. For the boot flow, `Game.claimOffline()` relies on `Game.meta.lastSeen`, which gets set when fresh metadata is created, and I'm updating it in the claim function — but I need to make sure `lastSeen` stays current during gameplay too.

There's a problem in `Game.loadMeta` where if the data version doesn't match the default, I'm merging fresh metadata with the old data, but `Object.assign` will let stale values from the old data override the fresh ones.

The `reviveFree` counter starts as the upgrade level, then decrements on free revives. Since `Game.reviveUsed` tracks both ad and free revives, and the revive button only shows when `reviveUsed === 0`, that's just one revive per run anyway — so the `m_rev` upgrade giving up to 3 free revives doesn't actually provide much value beyond the first one.

I need to fix this by tracking ad revives and free revives separately: `reviveAdUsed` and `reviveFreeUsed`. The logic becomes: show a free revive if `reviveFreeUsed < reviveFree`, otherwise show an ad revive if `reviveAdUsed === 0`. This way multiple free revives actually work as intended. I'll also update `Game.revive` to increment the right counter based on whether the revive was free.

/**
 * Headless smoke + balance test. Drives the REAL game in Chromium.
 * Run: node tools/playtest.mjs
 */
import { chromium } from 'playwright-core';
import { execSync, spawn } from 'node:child_process';
import { existsSync } from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';

const ROOT = path.resolve('..');
const PORT = 8123;
const BASE = `http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}`;

// serve the game folder
const server = spawn('python3', ['-m', 'http.server', String(PORT), '--bind', '0.0.0.0'], {
  cwd: ROOT, stdio: 'ignore',
});
await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 1200));

const errors = [];
const logs = [];

const exe = existsSync(process.env.PW_CHROMIUM || '') ? process.env.PW_CHROMIUM
  : execSync("ls -d ~/.cache/ms-playwright/chromium-*/chrome-linux/chrome 2>/dev/null | head -1").toString().trim();

const browser = await chromium.launch({ executablePath: exe, args: ['--no-sandbox', '--autoplay-policy=no-user-gesture-required'] });
const ctx = await browser.newContext({ viewport: { width: 412, height: 823 }, deviceScaleFactor: 2, isMobile: true, hasTouch: true });
const page = await ctx.newPage();

page.on('console', (m) => {
  const t = m.text();
  logs.push(`${m.type()}: ${t}`);
  if (m.type() === 'error') errors.push(t);
});
page.on('pageerror', (e) => errors.push('PAGEERROR: ' + e.message + '\n' + (e.stack || '')));

const results = {};

async function goto() {
  await page.goto(BASE + '/index.html', { waitUntil: 'load' });
  await page.waitForFunction(() => window.Game && window.Game.meta, null, { timeout: 15000 });
}

/* --------------------------------------------------------------- */
console.log('\n=== 1. BOOT (mobile viewport 412x823) ===');
await goto();
await page.waitForSelector('#scrMenu.active', { timeout: 15000 });
console.log('  ✓ menu visible, SDK state =', await page.evaluate(() => ({
  yandex: SDK.state.isYandex, lang: SDK.state.lang, device: SDK.getDeviceType(),
})));
results.bootOK = true;

/* --------------------------------------------------------------- */
console.log('\n=== 2. RUN + BALANCE (auto-picks upgrades, survives as long as it can) ===');
await page.click('#btnPlay');
await page.waitForFunction(() => Game.state === 'playing', null, { timeout: 5000 });

const timeline = [];
let lastLevel = 1;
let picks = 0;
let deathSeen = false;
let victorySeen = false;
const t0 = Date.now();

// Give the player godmode-ish survivability? No — we want the REAL curve.
// But we do want it to steer, so emulate a decent player: run away from the nearest enemy.
await page.evaluate(() => {
  window.__bot = setInterval(() => {
    if (Game.state !== 'playing') return;
    const p = Game.player;
    let ax = 0, ay = 0, n = 0;
    for (const e of Game.enemies) {
      if (e.dead) continue;
      const dx = p.x - e.x, dy = p.y - e.y;
      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
      if (d < 260 && d > 0.1) { ax += dx / d / d * 260; ay += dy / d / d * 260; n++; }
    }
    // drift toward xp gems
    let gx = 0, gy = 0, gd = 1e9;
    for (const g of Game.gems) {
      const d = Math.hypot(g.x - p.x, g.y - p.y);
      if (d < gd) { gd = d; gx = g.x; gy = g.y; }
    }
    if (gd < 400) { ax += (gx - p.x) / gd * 0.8; ay += (gy - p.y) / gd * 0.8; }
    const l = Math.hypot(ax, ay) || 1;
    Game.input.kx = ax / l; Game.input.ky = ay / l;
  }, 50);
});

while (Date.now() - t0 < 210000) {
  const st = await page.evaluate(() => window.__telemetry);
  if (!st) { await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 500)); continue; }

  if (st.level > lastLevel) {
    timeline.push({ t: st.t, level: st.level, enemies: st.enemies, hp: st.hp, weapons: st.weapons.slice() });
    lastLevel = st.level;
  }

  // handle level-up screen
  if (st.state === 'levelup') {
    const n = await page.locator('#offers .offer').count();
    if (n > 0) {
      // prefer evolution > rare weapon > rare passive > anything
      const idx = await page.evaluate(() => {
        const els = [...document.querySelectorAll('#offers .offer')];
        const evo = els.findIndex(e => e.classList.contains('evo'));
        if (evo >= 0) return evo;
        const rare = els.findIndex(e => e.classList.contains('rare'));
        return rare >= 0 ? rare : 0;
      });
      await page.locator('#offers .offer').nth(idx).click();
      picks++;
    }
    await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 60));
    continue;
  }

  if (st.state === 'gameover' || st.state === 'victory') {
    deathSeen = st.state === 'gameover';
    victorySeen = st.state === 'victory';
    results.final = st;
    break;
  }
  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 400));
}

const endState = await page.evaluate(() => ({
  state: Game.state, t: Math.round(Game.t), level: Game.level, score: Game.score,
  kills: Game.kills, biomass: Game.runBiomassResult, evolutions: Game.evolutionsRun,
  bosses: Game.bossKills,
  weapons: Object.entries(Game.weapons).map(([k, v]) => k + (v.evolved ? '★' : v.level)),
  passives: Object.entries(Game.passives).map(([k, v]) => k + v),
  peakEnemies: Game.enemies.length,
}));

console.log('\n  level-up timeline (t, level, enemies, hp):');
timeline.slice(0, 14).forEach(r => console.log(`    ${String(r.t).padStart(3)}s  L${String(r.level).padStart(2)}  enemies=${String(r.enemies).padStart(3)}  hp=${r.hp}  [${r.weapons.join(', ')}]`));
console.log(`    ... ${timeline.length} level-ups total, ${picks} picks made`);
console.log('\n  END:', JSON.stringify(endState, null, 2).replace(/\n\s*/g, ' '));
results.timeline = timeline;
results.end = endState;
results.deathSeen = deathSeen;
results.victorySeen = victorySeen;

/* --------------------------------------------------------------- */
console.log('\n=== 3. GAME OVER SCREEN + REVIVE (rewarded ad path) ===');
await page.waitForSelector('#scrOver.active', { timeout: 10000 });
const overVisible = await page.locator('#btnRevive').isVisible().catch(() => false);
console.log('  game-over screen shown ✓   revive button visible:', overVisible);
if (overVisible) {
  const label = (await page.locator('#btnRevive').innerText()).replace(/\n/g, ' | ');
  console.log('  revive label:', label);
  await page.click('#btnRevive');
  await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 2200)); // local SDK simulates a ~1.2s rewarded video
  const revived = await page.evaluate(() => ({ state: Game.state, hp: Math.round(Game.player.hp), revives: Game.reviveUsed }));
  console.log('  after rewarded ad ->', JSON.stringify(revived));
  results.revive = revived;
}
results.gameOverOK = true;

/* --------------------------------------------------------------- */
console.log('\n=== 4. META PERSISTENCE (cloud/local save round-trip) ===');
await page.evaluate(() => { Game.state = 'menu'; UI.toMenu(); });
await page.waitForSelector('#scrMenu.active', { timeout: 5000 });
const before = await page.evaluate(() => ({ biomass: Game.meta.biomass, best: Game.meta.bestScore, runs: Game.meta.runs }));
console.log('  before reload:', JSON.stringify(before));
await page.reload({ waitUntil: 'load' });
await page.waitForFunction(() => window.Game && window.Game.meta, null, { timeout: 15000 });
await page.waitForSelector('#scrMenu.active', { timeout: 10000 });
const after = await page.evaluate(() => ({ biomass: Game.meta.biomass, best: Game.meta.bestScore, runs: Game.meta.runs }));
console.log('  after  reload:', JSON.stringify(after));
results.persistOK = after.biomass === before.biomass && after.best === before.best;
console.log('  persistence:', results.persistOK ? '✓ OK' : '✗ MISMATCH');

/* --------------------------------------------------------------- */
console.log('\n=== 5. SCREENS SMOKE TEST ===');
for (const [btn, scr, back] of [
  ['#btnChars', '#scrChars', '#btnCharsBack'],
  ['#btnMeta', '#scrMeta', '#btnMetaBack'],
  ['#btnBoard', '#scrBoard', '#btnBoardBack'],
  ['#btnHelp', '#scrHelp', '#btnHelpBack'],
]) {
  await page.click(btn);
  await page.waitForSelector(scr + '.active', { timeout: 6000 });
  const items = await page.evaluate((s) => document.querySelectorAll(s + ' .card, ' + s + ' .brow, ' + s + ' .help').length, scr);
  console.log(`  ✓ ${scr} opened (${items} rows)`);
  await page.click(back);
  await page.waitForSelector('#scrMenu.active', { timeout: 6000 });
}
results.screensOK = true;

/* --------------------------------------------------------------- */
console.log('\n=== 6. UPGRADE PURCHASE ===');
await page.click('#btnMeta');
await page.waitForSelector('#scrMeta.active', { timeout: 5000 });
const buy = await page.evaluate(() => {
  const b0 = Game.meta.biomass;
  const before = JSON.stringify(Game.meta.upgrades);
  const ok = Game.buyUpgrade('m_dmg');
  return { ok, spent: b0 - Game.meta.biomass, changed: JSON.stringify(Game.meta.upgrades) !== before, upgrades: Game.meta.upgrades };
});
console.log('  buy m_dmg:', JSON.stringify(buy));
results.buyOK = buy.ok && buy.changed && buy.spent > 0;
await page.click('#btnMetaBack');

await page2.click('#btnPlay');

await page2.keyboard.up('KeyD');

results.keyboardOK = kx === 1;

await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 9000));

results.fpsOK = late && late.fps >= 45;

// Give the player godmode-ish survivability? No — we want the REAL curve.

  window.__bot = setInterval(() => {
    if (Game.state !== 'playing') return;
    const p = Game.player;
    let ax = 0, ay = 0, n = 0;
    for (const e of Game.enemies) {
      if (e.dead) continue;
      const dx = p.x - e.x, dy = p.y - e.y;
      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
      if (d < 260 && d > 0.1) { ax += dx / d / d * 260; ay += dy / d / d * 260; n++; }

    // drift toward xp gems
    let gx = 0, gy = 0, gd = 1e9;
    for (const g of Game.gems) {

      if (e.dead) continue;
      const dx = p.x - e.x, dy = p.y - e.y;
      const d = Math.hypot(dx, dy);
      const threat = d - e.r;
      if (threat < 90 && d > 0.1) {
        const w = (90 - threat) / 90;
        ax += (dx / d) * w * 4;
        ay += (dy / d) * w * 4;
        danger += w;
      }
    }

    // 2. attraction to the densest nearby gem cluster (this is what a player does)
    let bx = 0, by = 0, bw = 0;
    for (const g of Game.gems) {
      const d = Math.hypot(g.x - p.x, g.y - p.y);
      if (d < 520 && d > 0.1) {
        const w = (520 - d) / 520;
        bx += ((g.x - p.x) / d) * w;
        by += ((g.y - p.y) / d) * w;
        bw += w;
      }
    }
    if (bw > 0) { ax += bx * 1.6; ay += by * 1.6; }

    // 3. if nothing to do, drift outward so we don't sit in the middle of the horde
    if (bw === 0 && danger === 0) { ax += Math.cos(Game.t * 0.7); ay += Math.sin(Game.t * 0.9); }

    const l = Math.hypot(ax, ay) || 1;
    Game.input.kx = ax / l; Game.input.ky = ay / l;
  }, 40);
});

input.new_text:
  let canvas, ctx, W = 960, H = 600, dpr = 1;
  let bgDots = [];
  let time = 0;
  // Full-screen gradients are re-computed only on resize — filling them
  // every frame at 2x DPR was the single biggest frame cost.
  let bgLayer = null;
  let vignetteLayer = null;
  const MAX_PIXELS = 2.6e6; // cap the backing-store resolution

  function init(cv) {
    canvas = cv;
    ctx = cv.getContext('2d', { alpha: false });
    for (let i = 0; i < 90; i++) {
      bgDots.push({ x: Math.random() * 3000, y: Math.random() * 3000, r: Math.random() * 2.2 + 0.4, s: Math.random() * 0.5 + 0.2 });
    }
    resize();
    global.addEventListener('resize', resize);
    global.addEventListener('orientationchange', () => setTimeout(resize, 250));
  }

  function resize() {
    const rect = canvas.getBoundingClientRect();
    W = Math.max(320, rect.width);
    H = Math.max(240, rect.height);
    let d = Math.min(global.devicePixelRatio || 1, 2);
    const px = W * H * d * d;
    if (px > MAX_PIXELS) d = Math.sqrt(MAX_PIXELS / (W * H));
    dpr = Math.max(1, d);
    canvas.width = Math.floor(W * dpr);
    canvas.height = Math.floor(H * dpr);
    ctx.setTransform(dpr, 0, 0, dpr, 0, 0);
    Game.view.w = W; Game.view.h = H; Game.view.dpr = dpr;
    buildLayers();
  }

  /** Pre-render the static full-screen gradients once per resize. */
  function buildLayers() {
    bgLayer = document.createElement('canvas');
    bgLayer.width = Math.max(1, Math.floor(W)); bgLayer.height = Math.max(1, Math.floor(H));
    const b = bgLayer.getContext('2d');
    const g = b.createRadialGradient(W / 2, H / 2, 40, W / 2, H / 2, Math.max(W, H) * 0.85);
    g.addColorStop(0, '#0d2440');
    g.addColorStop(0.55, '#071a30');
    g.addColorStop(1, '#030d18');
    b.fillStyle = g;
    b.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);

    vignetteLayer = document.createElement('canvas');
    vignetteLayer.width = bgLayer.width; vignetteLayer.height = bgLayer.height;
    const v = vignetteLayer.getContext('2d');
    const vg = v.createRadialGradient(W / 2, H / 2, Math.min(W, H) * 0.42, W / 2, H / 2, Math.max(W, H) * 0.78);
    vg.addColorStop(0, 'rgba(0,0,0,0)');
    vg.addColorStop(1, 'rgba(0,0,0,0.55)');
    v.fillStyle = vg;
    v.fillRect(0, 0, W, H);
  }

import { chromium } from 'playwright-core';
import { spawn } from 'node:child_process';

const PORT = 8125;
const server = spawn('python3', ['-m', 'http.server', String(PORT), '--bind', '0.0.0.0'], { cwd: '/home/user/yandex-game', stdio: 'ignore' });
await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 1200));

const browser = await chromium.launch({
  executablePath: '/home/user/.cache/ms-playwright/chromium-1148/chrome-linux/chrome',
  args: ['--no-sandbox'],
});

async function measure(w, h, label, opts = {}) {
  const page = await browser.newPage({ viewport: { width: w, height: h } });
  page.on('pageerror', e => console.log('  PAGEERROR', e.message));
  await page.goto(`http://127.0.0.1:${PORT}/index.html`, { waitUntil: 'load' });
  await page.waitForFunction(() => window.Game && window.Game.meta, null, { timeout: 15000 });
  await page.click('#btnPlay');
  await page.waitForFunction(() => Game.state === 'playing', null, { timeout: 5000 });

  // build a heavy, deterministic scene
  await page.evaluate((o) => {
    Game.t = 600;
    for (let i = 0; i < o.enemies; i++) {
      const a = Math.random() * Math.PI * 2, d = 120 + Math.random() * 500;
      Game.enemies.push({
        type: 'drifter', def: CFG.ENEMY_TYPES.drifter,
        x: Game.player.x + Math.cos(a) * d, y: Game.player.y + Math.sin(a) * d,
        vx: 0, vy: 0, hp: 50, maxHp: 50, dmg: 10, r: 13, speed: 62, xp: 3,
        color: '#ff6b81', knockX: 0, knockY: 0, slowT: 0, slowAmt: 0,
        hitFlash: 0, cd: 1, wobbleA: Math.random() * 6, spawnT: 0, summonT: 99,
      });
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < o.particles; i++) {
      Game.particles.push({ x: Game.player.x + (Math.random() - .5) * 800, y: Game.player.y + (Math.random() - .5) * 600, vx: 1, vy: 1, life: 99, max: 99, color: '#fff', r: 3 });
    }
    for (let i = 0; i < o.gems; i++) {
      Game.gems.push({ x: Game.player.x + (Math.random() - .5) * 900, y: Game.player.y + (Math.random() - .5) * 700, vx: 0, vy: 0, v: 1, r: 4.5, pull: 0, life: 0 });
    }
    Game.state = 'paused';            // freeze simulation so we isolate render cost
    window.__frames = 0; window.__t0 = performance.now();
    const origRender = Render.render;
    Render.render = function (dt) { window.__frames++; return origRender(dt); };
  }, opts);

  await page.waitForTimeout(4000);
  const renderFps = await page.evaluate(() => ({
    fps: Math.round(window.__frames / ((performance.now() - window.__t0) / 1000)),
    enemies: Game.enemies.length, particles: Game.particles.length, gems: Game.gems.length,
  }));

  // now isolate simulation cost (render stubbed out)
  await page.evaluate(() => {
    const noop = () => {};
    Render.render = noop;
    Game.state = 'playing';
    Game.input.kx = 1;
    window.__frames2 = 0; window.__t1 = performance.now();
    window.__origUpd = Game.update;
  });
  await page.waitForTimeout(3000);
  const simFps = await page.evaluate(() => {
    const el = (performance.now() - window.__t1) / 1000;
    return { tps: Math.round(Game.frame / 1), elapsed: Math.round(el) };
  });

  console.log(`  ${label.padEnd(22)} render=${String(renderFps.fps).padStart(3)} fps  (scene: ${renderFps.enemies} enemies, ${renderFps.particles} particles, ${renderFps.gems} gems)`);
  await page.close();
  return renderFps.fps;
}

console.log('\n=== RENDER COST vs RESOLUTION (same scene, simulation frozen) ===');
const scene = { enemies: 250, particles: 400, gems: 300 };
const small = await measure(480, 320, '480x320', scene);
const mid = await measure(1024, 700, '1024x700', scene);
const big = await measure(1920, 1080, '1920x1080', scene);

console.log('\n=== SCENE COST at fixed 1024x700 ===');

console.log(`\n  NOTE: headless Chromium here uses SwiftShader (no GPU) -> canvas2d is pure CPU.`);
console.log(`  480x320 = ${(small)}fps vs 1920x1080 = ${(big)}fps. Ratio ${(small / big).toFixed(1)}x`);
console.log(`  If the ratio tracks pixel count (${((1920 * 1080) / (480 * 320)).toFixed(1)}x), the cost is fill-rate,`);
console.log(`  which real GPU-accelerated browsers handle far better.`);

await browser.close();
server.kill();

async function live(w, h, label, enemyCount) {

      Game.enemies.push({
        type: 'drifter', def: CFG.ENEMY_TYPES.drifter,
        x: Game.player.x + Math.cos(a) * d, y: Game.player.y + Math.sin(a) * d,

        hitFlash: 0, cd: 1, wobbleA: Math.random() * 6, spawnT: 0, summonT: 99,

  await page.close();
  return r.fps;

[reasoning]
Now I have precise data. The render IS expensive at 2.6MP: 41ms/frame. My earlier perf.mjs probe said 52-53fps at 1920x1080 — but that probe used `browser.newPage({viewport})` WITHOUT deviceScaleFactor, so dpr=1, giving a 1920x1080 = 2.07MP buffer... and it reported 60fps. Hmm, that contradicts.

Wait, in perf.mjs `live()` I set the loadout the same way. And it reported 60fps mobile / 56fps desktop. But profile.mjs says render alone is 24fps desktop.

Difference: profile.mjs uses `deviceScaleFactor: 2`. perf.mjs `live()` did NOT set deviceScaleFactor → defaults to 1. So:
- perf.mjs desktop 1440x900 dpr1 = 1.296MP → 56fps ✓
- profile.mjs desktop 1440x900 dpr2 → capped to 2.60MP → 24fps

So render cost scales steeply with pixel count in this software-rendered environment. 1.296MP → 56fps, 2.6MP → 24fps. That's roughly linear in pixels (2x pixels = 2.3x slower). So it IS fill-rate bound.

On real GPU-accelerated hardware, canvas2d fill is much faster. But I can't verify that here, and Yandex Games players include low-end Android devices where this matters. The honest engineering call: **reduce the pixel budget**. Dropping MAX_PIXELS from 2.6M to ~1.5M would roughly double render fps here with negligible visual difference on a phone.

Actually, better: cap DPR at 1.5 and cap MAX_PIXELS at 1.6M. Let me also reduce per-frame fill work:
- The plankton loop draws 90 arcs with globalAlpha changes. Cheap-ish.
- The grid: fine.
- Per-enemy glow arc: 231 extra arcs.

The dominant cost is likely the full-screen `drawImage` of bgLayer + vignetteLayer (2 × 2.6MP blits) plus the grid stroke and the 90 arcs — all full-screen-ish ops.

Let me test the hypothesis quickly by measuring render fps at different MAX_PIXELS. Actually simpler: just lower MAX_PIXELS to 1.6M and re-measure. If render fps roughly doubles, hypothesis confirmed.

Also `simulation: 16.67 ms/tick` — that's suspiciously exactly 16.67. That means the sim isn't the bottleneck at all; the measurement is just reflecting the rAF cadence (since the game loop is driven by rAF, when I stub render the loop still runs at 60fps and Game.update is called once per rAF... so simMs measures the rAF interval, not sim cost). My measurement is flawed — it measured frame cadence, not sim cost.

Let me measure sim cost properly: call Game.update(1/60) N times in a tight loop and time it.

Let me redo the profile properly:
- sim: time 300 direct Game.update(1/60) calls
- render: time 100 direct Render.render(1/60) calls

That's the correct way. Let me rewrite.

Then decide on the pixel budget.

Let me do it.

done

new = """    // --- measure simulation cost directly (N ticks in a tight loop) ---
    const origRender = Render.render;
    Render.render = () => {};
    Game.state = 'playing';
    for (let i = 0; i < 30; i++) Game.update(1 / 60);   // warm up

    t0 = performance.now();
    const M = 100;
    for (let i = 0; i < M; i++) Render.render(1 / 60);
    const renderMs = (performance.now() - t0) / M;

"""
s = s[:old_start] + new + s[old_end:]
s = s.replace("""      simMsPerTick: Math.round(simMs * 100) / 100,
      renderFps: Math.round(renderFps),
      renderMsPerFrame: Math.round((1000 / renderFps) * 100) / 100,""",
"""      simMsPerTick: Math.round(simMs * 100) / 100,
      renderMsPerFrame: Math.round(renderMs * 100) / 100,""")
s = s.replace("""  console.log(`  simulation : ${r.simMsPerTick} ms/tick  (budget 16.7)`);
  console.log(`  render     : ${r.renderFps} fps = ${r.renderMsPerFrame} ms/frame  (enemies=${r.enemies})`);""",
"""  console.log(`  simulation : ${r.simMsPerTick} ms/tick   (enemies=${r.enemies})`);
  console.log(`  render     : ${r.renderMsPerFrame} ms/frame`);""")
open(p,'w',encoding='utf-8').write(s); print("profile rewritten")

[reasoning]
Now I have clean data:
- **Simulation: 0.8–0.92 ms/tick** with 230 enemies. Excellent, not a bottleneck at all.
- **Render: 28.65 ms/frame at 2.60 MP** vs **16.41 ms/frame at 1.36 MP**. 

Ratio: 28.65/16.41 = 1.75x for 1.91x pixels. So render is essentially linear in pixel count → fill-rate bound.

Mobile (the primary Yandex audience) at 1.36MP gets 16.41ms render → 58fps combined. That's fine!

Desktop at 2.60MP gets 28.65ms → 34fps. Needs improvement. But note this is SwiftShader software rendering with no GPU. On a real machine with GPU-accelerated canvas, this would be far faster.

Still, the right engineering call is to reduce the pixel budget — it costs nothing visually at these sizes and helps low-end devices (which Yandex has plenty of). Let me lower MAX_PIXELS to 1.6M and cap dpr at 1.5. That should bring desktop to ~20ms render.

Let me also reduce redundant full-screen work:
- The `drawImage(bgLayer, 0, 0, W, H)` scales from a W×H canvas to W×H CSS px, but the backing store is W*dpr. So it's drawing a W×H image scaled up to W*dpr × H*dpr device pixels — that's a scaling blit of 2.6MP. Unavoidable if we want the gradient, but we could bake it at full buffer resolution instead. Actually drawImage with scaling is slower than 1:1. Let me build the layers at buffer resolution (canvas.width × canvas.height) and draw them 1:1 with the identity transform. That avoids scaling cost.

Let me do that: build layers at `canvas.width × canvas.height` and blit with `ctx.setTransform(1,0,0,1,0,0)` then `drawImage(layer, 0, 0)`. 1:1 blit is much cheaper.

Let me implement both:
1. MAX_PIXELS 2.6M → 1.7M, dpr cap 1.5
2. Bake layers at buffer resolution, blit 1:1

Let me apply and re-measure.

Game.chooseOffer = function (index) {

Game.offers = [];

Let me reduce the curve: xpForLevel(level) = floor(5 + level*2.2 + level^1.6*0.5)
- L1: 5+2.2+0.5=7
- L5: 5+11+0.5*5^1.6=5+11+6.6=22
- L10: 5+22+0.5*10^1.6=5+22+19.9=47
- L15: 5+33+0.5*15^1.6=5+33+38=76
- L20: 5+44+0.5*20^1.6=5+44+61=110
- L30: 5+66+0.5*30^1.6=5+66+118=189

Current: floor(6 + level*4 + level^1.85*0.9)
- L10: 6+40+0.9*70.8=6+40+63.7=110
- L15: 6+60+0.9*147=6+60+132=198
- L20: 6+80+0.9*254=6+80+229=315

So the new curve is ~2.3x cheaper at L10 and ~2.9x at L20. That's a big speedup. Combined with the XP buffs already made, leveling should be much faster.

p='js/ui.js'; s=open(p,encoding='utf-8').read(); o=s
old = """  function renderLoadout() {
    const box = $('loadout');
    if (!box) return;
    let html = '';
    for (const id of Object.keys(Game.weapons)) {
      const w = Game.weapons[id];
      const def = CFG.WEAPONS[id];
      const ev = w.evolved ? CFG.EVOLUTIONS[id].result : null;
      const color = ev ? ev.color : def.color;
      html += '<div class="chip' + (ev ? ' ev' : '') + '" style="color:' + color + '">' +
        '<i style="background:' + color + '"></i>' +
        '<span style="color:#e8f6ff">' + (ev ? ev.name : def.name) + '</span>' +
        '<span style="color:' + color + '">' + (ev ? 'MAX' : w.level) + '</span></div>';
    }
    for (const id of Object.keys(Game.passives)) {
      const def = CFG.PASSIVES[id];
      html += '<div class="chip" style="color:' + def.color + '">' +
        '<i style="background:' + def.color + '"></i>' +
        '<span style="color:#e8f6ff">' + def.name + '</span>' +
        '<span style="color:' + def.color + '">' + Game.passives[id] + '</span></div>';
    }
    box.innerHTML = html;
  }"""
new = """  function renderLoadout() {
    const box = $('loadout');
    if (!box) return;
    let html = '';
    let bestHint = null;
    for (const id of Object.keys(Game.weapons)) {
      const w = Game.weapons[id];
      const def = CFG.WEAPONS[id];
      const ev = w.evolved ? CFG.EVOLUTIONS[id].result : null;
      const color = ev ? ev.color : def.color;
      let right = '' + (ev ? 'MAX' : w.level);
      let cls = ev ? ' ev' : '';

      // Show progress toward the evolution so the player always has a visible goal.
      const evDef = CFG.EVOLUTIONS[id];
      if (evDef && !w.evolved) {
        const req = evDef.requires;
        const pl = Game.passives[req.passive] || 0;
        const wDone = w.level >= req.weaponLevel;
        const pDone = pl >= req.passiveLevel;
        if (wDone && pDone) {
          cls += ' ready';
          right = 'ГОТОВО';
          bestHint = { text: def.name + ' → ' + evDef.result.name + ': возьмите это улучшение!', color: evDef.result.color };
        } else {
          right = w.level + '/' + req.weaponLevel;
          const need = [];
          if (!wDone) need.push(def.name + ' ' + w.level + '\\u2192' + req.weaponLevel);
          if (!pDone) need.push(CFG.PASSIVES[req.passive].name + ' ' + pl + '\\u2192' + req.passiveLevel);
          const score = (w.level / req.weaponLevel) + (pl / req.passiveLevel);
          if (!bestHint || score > bestHint.score) {
            bestHint = { score, text: '\\u2605 ' + evDef.result.name + ': ' + need.join(' + '), color: def.color };
          }
        }
      }

      html += '<div class="chip' + cls + '" style="color:' + color + '">' +
        '<i style="background:' + color + '"></i>' +
        '<span style="color:#e8f6ff">' + (ev ? ev.name : def.name) + '</span>' +
        '<span style="color:' + color + '">' + right + '</span></div>';
    }
    for (const id of Object.keys(Game.passives)) {
      const def = CFG.PASSIVES[id];
      html += '<div class="chip" style="color:' + def.color + '">' +
        '<i style="background:' + def.color + '"></i>' +
        '<span style="color:#e8f6ff">' + def.name + '</span>' +
        '<span style="color:' + def.color + '">' + Game.passives[id] + '</span></div>';
    }
    if (bestHint) {
      html += '<div class="chip evo-hint' + (bestHint.text.indexOf('ГОТОВО') >= 0 ? '' : '') + '" style="color:' + bestHint.color + '">' +
        '<span style="color:' + bestHint.color + '">' + bestHint.text + '</span></div>';
    }
    box.innerHTML = html;
  }"""
assert old in s
s=s.replace(old,new)
open(p,'w',encoding='utf-8').write(s); print("evolution tracker added")
PY

                  traps: Game.traps.length, novae: Game.novae.length, orbiters: Game.orbiters.length,

  const simMs = (performance.now()-t)/300;
  Game.state = 'paused'; Render.render = or;
  for (let i=0;i<10;i++) Render.render(1/60);
